vidhirasAyanam (sukhopayojinIsahitam)
appayyadIkSitaH

Source: Vidhirasayanadigranthapancakam (Srimad Appayya Deekshithendra Granthavali Series 8, Hyderabad 2007), OCR, corrected.
Transliteration: Kyoto-Harvard. [p.N] = page of the printed edition.

== Root verses (mUlazlokAH) ==

[p.1] vidhirasAyanasya mUlazlokAH
prakaraNArthapratijJA
vikhyAtA munivaryasUktiSu vidhAstisro vidhisrotasAm AcAryairvizadaM viviktaviSayAstAzca vyavasthApitAH | kiM tatrA'sti vicAryamAryamathite mArge nisargojjvale nAnodAharaNaistu tAH pravizadIkartuM pravartAmahe ||1||
apUrvavidhilakSaNAkSepaH
atyantAprAptiyukte vidhiriti viSaye saptamI varNyate cet avyAptiH prAptiyuktaM viSayamupagateSvagnividhyAdiSu syAt | agre dRSTvaiva saptamyaviSamagataye yojyate cet tryanIkAbhAjoragratvavidhyoH prathamapadajuSoH syAttadAvyAptidoSaH ||2||
aprAptiM prAptibhAjAmapi yadi paramAvAntarApUrvakRttvavyAvRttoddezyadharmaiH sRjasi vighaTayannenamavyAptidoSam | tasmAdAdAya lakSmakSayakRti nihitaM syAdativyAptivaktre vrIhyuddezo'pi hantau niyamavidhipade khalvapUrvAvalIDhaH ||3||
yUpaH sarvo'pyapUrvaH svayamapi vividhAdRSTasaMskArayogAt tasmin daikSe'pi dRSTaM khadiraniyamanaM tatra kA prAptivArtA | tasmAttattatsvarUpe paramanavagataM rUpabhedaM vihAya prAptyaprAptI vicArye kathamapi tadihA'vyAptidoSAnna muktiH ||4||
avyAptiH paurNamAsyAmapi yajanavidhAvatra kalpe prasarpet prAptoddezyasya tena hyadhigataphalatopAMzuyAjasya labhyA | syAtkiJcaiSA''parAgnyAhutiSu phalasamAyojanArthapravRtteH niSpratyUhAgnihotrAhutiyugalamitAdhArasaGkIrtanepi ||5||
[p.2]
AkAGkSyAdarthavAdAdupanamati phale karmaNo yadvidhAnam tatrA'pyeSA tadaGgIbhavanakRti bhavetsannidhAnazrutAnAm | yatkiJcidyatra kutrApyanadhikRtavatItyeva lakSmasthitizcet hantyAdyeSu prasiddho niyamavidhirapi syAdapUrvaM vidhAnam ||6||
uddezye'nyatra vA yadvidhipadamitaradvA'pi na prAptimatsyAt tatprAptiM tatra tanvanvidhiriti karaNe'pyevamavyAptireva | jyotiSTomAdikAnAM tridivagatiphale sarvakAmoktisiddhe kiM prApyaM dharmajAte prakaraNagamite svargakAmazrutibhyAm ||7||
nAnAzAkhAgnihotraprabhRtividhiSu cA'vyAptirevaM sati syAt tattadvidhyapravRttAvapi tadadhigatiH syAddhi vidhyantareNa | sA sA prAptistu naivA'bhyudayaphalavatItyevamaprAptisattvam kRtvA'vyApternirAse na niyama-parisaMkhyAnuvRtternivRttiH ||8||
tattadvidhyapravRttau sulabhamitaravidhyapravRttyA yutAyAm teSAmaprAptimattvaM niyamapadayava-vrIhipakSAptitAvat | nAnAkarmopasaMhRtyucitaniyatatatsAptadazyAptivaccetyevaM sRSTau tu pRSThastutiniyamamukhAtiprasaktiH prasarpet ||9||
yadyekAnekavidhyaprasRtimukhabhavaH saGkaro lakSaNAnAm iSyetA'nyAzrayatvaprathama-caramatAdyuktasAGkaryarItyA | daikSAdanyo'pi daNDaH prakRtijuSi bhavetpreSakArye prazAstuH syAtpakSe stotrasAmnAM kratunikarajuSAmuttarAsveva gAnam ||10||
kiJcA'prApte satIti prabhavati parisaMkhyAnivRttiM na kartuM nityAprAptAM nivRttiM ghaTayati khalu sA'pyatra ko vA vizeSaH | bhAvAprAptiniSedhAtmakavidhinikaravyApakAvyAptiduSTA satyapyevaM niSedhazrutimukhaparisaMkhyAsu cA'tiprasaktiH ||11||
yenA'tivyAptibhaGgaH kila niyamavidhau kiM tadAtyantikatvaM sarvoddezyAnuvRttiryadi punaruditAvyAptayaH syurvikalpe | prAptoddezyaikadezA api khalu kuhaciccAritArthyaM bhajantaH zrUyante sArvakAmyaprabhRtiSu vidhayasteSu cA'vyAptayaH syuH ||12||
uddezyavyApikA sA yadi bhavati tadA''pyAdimAvyAptibandhaH syAdavyAptizca gAvAmayanikaSaDahe pRSThasAmnorvidhAne | anyArthaprApaNodyanniyama-haraNayorapyativyAptirittham taccet traikAlikatvaM na tu vidhirapavAdApavAdastathA syAt ||13||
yujyetA'pAkSikatvaM taditi yadi nirucyeta tatpAkSikatvaM prAptyA kimmIritatvaM kathitatadadhikAvyAptiduSTaM na yuktam | tyaktvA'vacchedabhedaM taditi tu niyamAtiprasaktyA nirAsyaM tatropAyAntaraM tadviraha iti tayorhAstyavacchedabhedaH ||14||
tadbhinnatvaM nivezyaM yadi khalu tadavacchedabhede tato na vyAvRttiH kAryaSoDazyRtugamanabRhatsaMzritAyA niyatyAH | sthAne vaikalpikoktiH kriyata iti mataM yadyupAyAntarANAm tadvAcyaM pAkSikatvavyatikararahitaM kintu vaikalpikatvam ||15||
yadyucyetA'ntareNa tvadhikaraNabhidA prAptisaMmelanaM tat tatrA'pi prAkSu pakSeSvapi samudayate tAvadavyAptirekA | kArtsnyenaivopahavyaprabhRtibhRtihavizzeSayorbrahmaNoktau pUtIkAdhAramantrAntaramukhaniyamAtiprasaktistathA'nyA ||16||
[p.4]
niyamavidhilakSaNAkSepaH
hetuH kutrA'pi kAryaM kvacana tadubhayaM kutracidvidhyupAttam vidhyaspRSTaM niyamyaM kvacidubhayavidhaM pAkSikatvena dRSTam | tasyaiva pratyanIkaM kvacidapi kuhacitpAkSikaM naiva dRSTam nAnArUpaM taditthaM niyamavidhizataM durgrahaM lakSaNena ||17||
taccA'tivyAptamagnervikRtijuSi vidhau mIlanasyA'gratAyAH naimittikyAzca vidhyorakhilavidhiSu ca prApakAvartakeSu | aprAptAMzasya pUrtiH phalamapi yadi tatrA'dhikaM yojanIyaM zeSAMzavyarthatA syAdakhilaphalakatAvidhyativyApanaM ca ||18||
aprAptatvaM virodhyAkramaNakRtamihA'pekSyate yadyathA'pi syAddoSo'yaM hi pRSThyaH SaDaha iti vidhAvapyayaM nirnirodhaH | aprAptAMzaM vizeSTuM kriyata iti mataM yadyanirdhAritatvam zyAvAzvabrAhmaNoktiprabhRtividhigaNAtiprasaktistathA'pi ||19||
parisaMkhyAvidhilakSaNAkSepaH
tatrA'nyatreti rAzidvitayamabhihitaM lakSaNe pArisaMkhye pazyAmastatra lakSyopagatamanugataM bhinnadharmopagUDham | pAThaprAptAgratAvidhyanugatirahitaM vidhyupAttAnupAttaM grAhyaM vyAvartanIyaM tadapi ca na bhajatyAvapantIti lakSyam ||20||
prAptiH zeSadvaye cediha vividiSitAzeSayugme na sA syAt avyAptistvAzrayAdidvitayajuSi bhaveccheSazeSitvatazcet | yatkiJcidyogatazcet bahuSaDahazatokthyAdividhyAkramaH syAt sarvatra brAhmaNoktipramukhavidhiSu cA'tiprasaktiH samAnA ||21||
tatrA'nyatrA'pi cA''ptirniyamavidhiSu cAstItyativyAptireva nityaprAptirvivakSyA na khalu virahataH kUrmakIzAdibhakSye | nityA'nityA'pi vA'sminkathamapi ca samuccitya cetprAptiriSTA patnIsaMyAjAntatAyAM niyamakRtividhau sA tathA'pyapraNodyA ||22||
yojyastatraiva bAdhaH phalamapi yadi tadvA'rtha evA'vaziSTaH tasyA'pyuktyA niSedhAtmakavidhiniyamAtiprasakterarodhAt | prAptaM sarvatra kiJcitkhalu sulabhataraM varjanIyena sAkaM gRhyante te'pi lakSyA yadi tadapi vidhau sarvathAtiprasaktiH ||23||
trividhavidhilakSaNAkSepasya parasparAtidezaH
pUrvAkSepaprakAraH prabhavati paramapyAtmavazyaM vidhAtum prAyaH sUkSmaM vibhAgaM vidhiSu gamayatAM lakSaNAnAmamISAm | zakyaM tannyAyasAmyaM praNihitamatibhirboddhamityapradarzya triSvapyeteSu bhinnasthitiSu navanavo varNitastatprakAraH ||24||
[p.6] apUrvaniyamaparisaMkhyodAharaNAkSepaH
utpazyAmo na codAharaNagaNamapi prAptarUpaM vidhInAM prAptaM khalvagnidIkSAhavanamiva haviH prokSaNaM mantraliGgAt | naiyatyaM somapAtragrahaNa iva tataH prAptamevA'vahantau prAptaM taddRSTakAryAnvayaparamusalolUkhalAmnAnatazca ||25||
tasmAdatrobhayasmin yavaparihRtaye pArisaMkhyo vidhiH syAt nocetprAptyai niyatyai kathamapi ca bhavanna prasiddho'pi saH syAt | yadvA'pUrvo vidhiH syAnniyamapara ihA''dhvaryavatvaM niyacchan anyasmindvandvasampAdanavidhivazage tatprayacchannapUrvaH ||26||
evaJcA'zvAbhidhAnIgrahavidhirapi tatkartRnaiyatyamAtraprAptyarthaH paryavasyetprathanavidhiriva prAptamantrAnuvAdI | mantre'pyanyAprasaktyai vidhimiha saguNaprAkRtAGgoktanItiH na kSAmyenmantramAtre sati vidhiviSaye syAdviziSTAnuvAdaH ||27||
uddezyo'trA'zva evetyapi na phalavati spaSTalabhye vidheye bhAvArthe ko hi vidvAnvidhimiha tamupekSyA'nyasaMkrAntimicchet | karturnaiyatyametatphalamapi parisaMkhyAvasAyyastu nAma spRSTA naiSA hi doSairna ca bhavati vidhervidhyanarhopayAnam ||28||
yavrIhiprokSaNAderyavamapi viSayaM tanvate tacca cintyam yuktA'pUrveNa dadhyAnayanavidhinayAdarthavattA vidhInAm | no cennaimittiko'pi pratinidhivadiyAtsomadharmopadezAn RcchetsammIlanAdInapi ca bRhadapi stutyapUrvAvibhedAt ||29||
mantreNA'zvasya rajjorgrahaNavidhiphalaM mantrabAdho'nyarajjau traidoSyaM prAptabAdhe prasaraNamayate na tvihetyapyayuktam | AgneyInyAyatastAmapi punarupayankena mantro nivAryaH traidoSyaM cA''jyakAryAntaramukhaparisaMkhyAsu kenopapAdyam ||30||
atrA''pyAkSepavarge katicana zithilAH zAstramaryAdayA te doSAssyurlakSaNodAharaNagaNa iti pratyapAdyanta pUrvam | tatra sthAlIpulAkaprathitanayadizA keSu ciddarzayAmaH zaithilyaM tadviruddhaM sakalamiva bhavadyena dRzyeta tantram ||31||
apUrvaniyamaparisaMkhyodAharaNAkSepasamAdhAnam
pazvAdyartho hi naiva prakRtiSu vikRtiSvadhvareSvagniraGgam kA tatrAmUlabhagnAtkathaya vikRtiSu prAptivArtA'tidezAt | agniSTomAdivAkyAni tu phalajananAyA''zrayArthAni liGgAt tatprAptirna prakRtyAM na ca vikRtiSu sA tadvidhau tatra vRtteH ||32||
nityAtkAmyasya zaighryAtprakRtimapi samApadyatAM liGgato'gniH so'GgaM saMsthAdyavAkyaiH prakRti-vikRtiSu syAdvirAtrAdigo'pi | tadvAkyAnyAdyavAkyaistribhirapi nikhilAhargaNAnprApite'gnau kiM kurvantvagatyA prakRtamiha vidhivrAtamagneH stuvantu ||33||
agniSTomAdayazcetprakRtaya iha na syAtsamAnaM vidhAnaM saGkocazca zrutInAM vacananayavidAM nirnimitto na yuktaH | tasmAdavyAptiragnervidhiSu na ghaTate varNitA vaikRteSvityAkSepe'sminpravaktuM nayasaraNicaNaiH zakyametAvadeva ||34||
[p.8]
agniSTomAdizabdAH prakRtivikRtisaMsparzinassantu nAma prApyatvAmnAtasaMsthAnprathamamatha na te yAntyanAmnAtasaMsthAn | tattattyaktadvirAtraprabhRtiSu vidhayazcodakaprAptamagni zyenAkRtyAdilabdhyai vidadhata iti teSvakSatAvyAptateti ||35||
tatraitaddUSaNaM syAnna guNaphalavidhiSvatra vA'nyatra vA'gniH prAkkiJcitkratvapUrvAnvayamupagatavAnAzrayo'trAnuvAdyaH | kintvagniSTomamukhyakratusamadhigamAtprAganArabhyavAde zuddho'gnistasya naiva kvacidapi vikRtAvAzrayatve'sti bhedaH ||36||
vAcastvagnerdvirAtraprabhRtiSu parisaJcakSatAmanyato'gnim teSvanyAGgAni yadvA niyamanamatha vA teSu kurvantu tasya | yeSvetAH kliSTagatyA guNaphalabhavanaprApaNArthA bhaveyuH SoDazyartheSviva syAttadapi vidhiphalaM teSvavazyaM tathA'pi ||37||
ukthyAdispRkphalAnAM kratusamadhigatiM manvate ye'pi teSAm tenA'gniSTomasaMsthakratuvikRtitayokthyAdisaMsthA na sidhyet | kintu syAtsArvakAmyoktyuditaphalabhidAsAdhikAyAM prakRtyAm ukthyAdInAM vyavasthA prakaraNapaThanAtsarvatastatprasaktau ||38||
tAsu syAtsAptadazyaM prakaraNarahitaM yAsu na zrUyate tattAsvasya syAdvikalpo vikRtiSu kathite codakAtpAJcadazye | satyevaM mitravindAdiSu niyamavidhistasya bhUyo vidhissyAt sArthakye sambhavatyabhyudayaphalakatA kevalaM naiva kalpyA ||39||
nAstyevopAMzuyAjo dadhizRtasahitaH paurNamAsyAM vidhAnAt tadvazyaM darzatantre paThitamapi RgAmnAtayAjyAnuvAkyam | kRtvAcintaiva tasya kvacidadhikaraNe dArzikeSvastitoktiH tattredhA taNDulAnityanuvadanamatastatra nA'vyAptirasti ||40||
pratyekaM dravyayugme na paTu ghaTayituM devatAM vAkyamekaM sArUpyasyA'pi bhaGgaH pazuphalavacasA kevalaM tadvidhAne | tasmAttAvadviziSTAnvayini sati vidhAvArtivAkyoktarItyA vaktavyo devatAnAmapanayanavidhiH kalpanaM kvopayuktam ||41||
tarhi syAddevatAbhyaH pravibhajanavidhistatra tadgAminIbhyo na syAdevaM hi sarvAsvapi dadhizRtayordevatAsvanvayaH syAt | tasminvAkye vidheyaM vibhajanamiha cedvakSyamANaprakAraM na syAdaupAMzuyAje haviSi taditi tatsarvathA vyarthameva ||42||
niyamaparisaMkhyApUrvavidhInAM niSkRSTalakSaNAni
evaM prApte bhaNAmastrividhavidhijuSAM lakSaNAnAM vibhAgaM dRSTArtho yaH sa sarvo bhavati niyama ityabhyupetya vyavasthAm | arthAdvA zabdato vA svaviSayagalitAdevakArAnnaJo vA yaH syAtkasyApi bAdhaH sa bhavati parisaMkhyeti rItiM ca kRtvA ||43||
nityaudAsyAnyakiJcidvigalitaniyataM yasya tatprAptimAtraM tatkAlaM yasya bodhaH samudayati nivRttyAtmanA'vazyameva | prAptiM bhinnasya tAbhyAM kathamapi vidadhatsyAdapUrvo vidhirnaH kurvanprAptiM tayoH syAtsa niyamaparisaMkhyAbhidhAnaH krameNa ||44||
samAptA mUlazlokAH

== Root verses with commentary (sukhopayojinI) ==

[p.9] vyAkhyAnazlokAH udghATya yogakalayA hRdayAbjakozam dhanyaizcirAdapi yathAruci gRhyamANaH | yaH prasphuratyavirataM paripUrNarUpaH zreyaH sa me dizatu zAzvatikaM mukundaH ||1|| janma-sthema-layaM samastajagatAM yasyaiva lIlAyitam yasminnA''yatate bhavasthiramahApAzakSayaH prANinAm | tadbrahmAdibhirapyagamyavibhavaM vizvAdhikaM zAzvatam sAmbaM brahmarasAlamUlarasikaM pazyeyamantardRzA ||2|| yatkumArilamatAnusAriNA nirmitaM vidhirasAyanaM mayA | padyarUpamanatisphuTAzayaM tatsukhAvagataye vivicyate ||3|| prAptaM tatprApaNIyaM kila yadiha kiyAnpUraNIyo'sti nAMzo nA'niSTaM vA nivartyaM nijaviSayatayA dRzyate kiJcidasya | kintu vyApArameva prathayati phalasaMyojanArthaM pareSAm prAptaH puNyairagaNyairiva vibudhagaNo veGkaTakSoNipAlam ||4|| vilakSaNAnyapyuditAni lakSaNAnyapUrvavidhyAdivibhAgasiddhaye | bhavanti bhUmneha kRtAM padAvaliM parasparAmapyaparasparAmimAm ||5|| vyAkhyAnazlokAH samAptAH zrImadappayyadIkSitendraviracitam svopajJasukhopayojinIsahitam sukhopayojinI-vyAkhyA udghATya yogakalayA hRdayAbjakozam dhanyaizcirAdapi yathAruci gRhyamANaH | yaH prasphuratyavirataM paripUrNarUpaH zreyaH sa me dizatu zAzvatikaM mukundaH ||1|| janma-sthema-layaM samastajagatAM yasyaiva lIlAyitam yasminnA''yatate bhavasthiramahApAzakSayaH prANinAm | tadbrahmAdibhirapyagamyavibhavaM vizvAdhikaM zAzvatam sAmbaM brahmarasAlamUlarasikaM pazyeyamantardRzA ||2|| yatkumArilamatAnusAriNA nirmitaM vidhirasAyanaM mayA | padyarUpamanatisphuTAzayaM tatsukhAvagataye vivicyate ||3|| [p.11] prAripsitaprakaraNAvighnasamAptaye sUtrakArasya maharSeranusmaraNarUpaM maGgalamAcaranneva prakaraNArambhaM tAvadAkSipya samAdhatte---- prakaraNArthapratijJA vikhyAtA munivaryasUktiSu vidhAstisro vidhisrotasAm AcAryairvizadaM viviktaviSayAstAzca vyavasthApitAH | kiM tatrA'sti vicAryamAryamathite mArge nisargojjvale nAnodAharaNaistu tAH pravizadIkartuM pravartAmahe ||1|| [p.12] munivaryasya-bhagavato jaimineH sUtreSu "vidhirvA syAdapUrvatvAt" (mI0 sU0 1|2|2|19) "niyamArthA vA zrutiH" (4|2|9|24) "parisaMkhyA" (1|2|4|34) ityevamAdiSu trayo vidhiprakArAH prasiddhAH apUrvavidhiH, niyamavidhiH, parisaMkhyAvidhizceti | te ca bhaTTapAdaiH parasparAsaMkIrNaviSayatayA spaSTamevopapAditAH | tatra svata eva niravadye granthakAraizca bahubhirniSkarSaM prApite'pUrvavidhyAdivibhAge nUtanaM kiJcidvicAraNIyaM nAstyeva; yadyapyevam, tathApyapUrvavidhyAdInAM dvAdazasvapyadhyAyeSu viprakIrNairihApUrvavidhyAdilakSaNavicAravyAjena tatra-tatra pradarzanIyairudAharaNavizeSaistamevArthamatispaSTIkartuM prakaraNamidamArabhyata ityarthaH ||1|| iti prathamazlokasya vyAkhyA mantrAdhikaraNasthavArtikoktatrividhavidhivivaraNam | atha-"vidhiratyantamaprApte niyamaH pAkSike sati | tatra cA'nyatra ca prApte parisaMkhyeti gIyate ||" iti zlokena''cAryairapUrvavidhyAdInAM lakSaNamukhena vibhAgaH kRtaH | tatra '"vrIhIn prokSatI"tyatrApUrvavidhiH | etadabhAve hi dArzapaurNamAsikeSu vrIhiSu prokSaNaM kathamapi na prApnoti | "vrIhInavahantIti" niyamavidhiH | etadabhAve puroDAzArthamupAtteSu vrIhiSu tuSavimoke lokasiddhakAraNatvAt, tadAkSiptatayA'vaghAtaH prApnotyeva; kintu lokasiddhakAraNatvAvizeSAtkadAcinnakhavidalanamapi prApnotIti pAkSikI tasyA''kSepataH prAptiH| ""imAmagRbhNan razanAmRtasyetyazvAbhidhAnImAdatta" iti parisaMkhyAvidhiH | etadabhAve'pyagRbhNan razanAmiti liGgAdagnicayanaprakaraNAmnAto mantrastatratyAzvarazanAgrahaNe niyatameva prApnoti; kintu liGgAvizeSAttatratyagardabharazanAgrahaNe'pi prApnotIti tatra cA'nyatra ca prAptiH | niyama-parisaMkhyayorapyapUrvavidhitvApAdanam [p.13] yadyapi niyama-parisaMkhyodAharaNayoH pAkSikI, niyatA ca prAptirvastuto nAstyeva; tuSavimokena kAryeNA''kSiptatayA hyavaghAtasya pAkSikI prAptirvaktavyA; AkSepAtprAgeva "vrIhInavahantIti" pratyakSazAstraM jhaTiti pravartate | tadanantaraM pratyakSazAstrasiddhenaikenopAyena nairAkAGkSyAdaniyatopAyAkSepa eva na pravartate | evaM pratyakSazAstreNA'zvarazanAgrahaNe jhaTiti viniyuktasya mantrasya tenaiva nairAkAGkSyAdrazanAdvayasAdhAraNyena laiGgikaviniyogakalpanaM na pravartate | evaJca niyama-parisaMkhyayorapyapUrvavidhivadvastuto'tyantA'prApta eva viSayaH | phaladvArA'pUrvavidhito niyama-parisaMkhyayorbhedanirUpaNam tathApi niyama-parisaMkhyAzAstrayorapravRttau arthAkSepeNa nakhavidalanasyA'pi kadAcitprAptyA'vaghAtasyA'pi pAkSikyeva prAptiH syAt | mantrasya liGgAvizeSeNa gardabharazanAgrahaNe'pi viniyogaH syAt | tayoH pravRttau tvAkSepa-mantraliGgAvatArapratibandhadvArA'vaghAtasya niyatatA; mantrasya gardabharazanAto nivRttizca labhyata iti niyamaparisaMkhyAvidhyoH pravRttAvapUrvavidhyavizeSe'pyAkSepamantraliGgAvatArapratibandhadvArA'vaghAtaniyamena; gardabharazanAyAM mantravarjanena ca phalavattvAtphalato niyamaparisaMkhyAvyapadeza iti; apUrvavidhitastayorbhedaH | itaravyAvRtterevakAreNa phalavivaraNasya caikyena niyama-parisaMkhyayoH sAGkaryazaGkA-parihArau yadyapi niyame nakhavidalanAdinivRttiH parisaMkhyAyAM gardabharazanAdinivRttiH, itItaranivRttilAbha ubhayatrA'pyaviziSTaH; avaghAtenaiva tuSavimokaH kAryaH, azvarazanAyAmeva mantraH kArya iti; evakAreNa phalavivaraNamapyaviziSTam, tathA'pyavaghAtazAstreNA'vaghAtaniyamasyA''kSepato'vaghAtAprApyavrIhyaMzeSvavaghAtaprApaNarUpasyA'karaNe upAyAntarasAdhAraNenA''kSepeNA''patata upAyAntarasya nirAkartu[p.14]mazakyatayA tannivRttyasambhavena niyamasya prathamopasthitatvAt; vidheyagatatvena pratyAsannatvAccA'prAptAMzaprApaNarUpo niyama eva phalam | upAyAntaranivRttistu caramopasthitatvAt, avidheyagatatvena viprakRSTatvAcca tadanuniSpAdinI na phalam | parisaMkhyodAharaNe tu dvayorapi nityaprAptisaMbhavena svarUpaprApteH; niyamasya vA phalatvAyogAditaranivRttireva phalam | evaM niyama-parisaMkhyAvidhyoH [p.20] tRtIyazlokasyA'vatArikA [p.21] syAdetat-satisaptamIpakSe prathamavikalpe udbhAvitamidamavyAptidvayaM na prasajyate | tathA hi-dvAdazAhatryanIkAntargatamagratAdvayaM paramApUrvasAdhanatvopahitayorAdyayorahnorvidhIyate | na ca tathAbhUtayostayoH prakRtitastadagratAdvayaM prAptam; kintvavAntarApUrvasAdhanatvopahitayoreva; dvAdazAnAM somayAgAnAmekaphalAvacchinnAnAM sahAnuSThAne'pi pratisomayAgaM codakasya pRthak pravRtteH | tathA gAvAmayanikayorAdyayorahnoravAntarApUrvasAdhanatvopahitayoraindravAyavAgratAdvayaM vidhIyate | na ca tathAbhUtayostatprAptam; kintu paramApUrvasAdhanatvopahitayoreva | idaJca tattadavyAptyudbhAvanasthale spaSTIkRtam | tathA coddezyatAvacchedakarUpeNA'prAptisattvAdudbhAvitAvyAptyornA'vakAza ityAzaGkyA''ha- aprAptiM prAptibhAjAmapi yadi paramAvAntarApUrvakRttvavyAvRttoddezyadharmaiH sRjasi vighaTayannenamavyAptidoSam | tasmAdAdAya lakSmakSayakRti nihitaM syAdativyAptivaktre vrIhyuddezo'pi hantau niyamavidhipade khalvapUrvAvalIDhaH ||3|| yadyavyAptiM parihartuM prAptAnAmapyagratAnAmapUrvavizeSasAdhanatvopAdhibhedena aprAptiM lakSaNArthatvena kalpayasi, tadA keSAM cideva prasiddhAnAmapUrvavidhInAM saMgrAhakamidaM lakSaNamuktamiti lakSyasaMkocavivakSayA supariharAdavyAptidoSAdAdAya lakSaNahAnikarasya niyamavidhyativyAptidoSavyAghrasya vadane idaM lakSaNaM tvayA nihitaM syAt | "vrIhInavahantI"ti niyamavidhAvapi vrIhINAmapUrvasAdhanatvopahitAnAmevoddezyatayA uddezyatAvacchedakarUpeNA'prAptisattvAt | tatra hi dvitIyAzrutyA vrIhINAmavaghAtasAdhyatvaM pratIyate, na ca teSAM vrIhitvena rUpeNa tatsAdhyatvamasti; prAgeva siddhatvAt | nA'pi "vrIhInavahantI"ti laukikavrIhyavahanAnuvAdavAkya ivA'tra tuSavimokaviziSTatvena rUpeNa tatsAdhyatvaM dvitIyArtha iti vaktuM zakyam | vidalanenA'pi sambhavati tasmin atra vivakSitasyA'vaghAtaniyamasyA'nupayogAt | [p.22] tasmAdapUrvasAdhanatvAkAreNaiva teSAM tatsAdhyatvaM vAcyam | na ca yathA "agnInAdadhIte" tyatrA'gnInAM svarUpeNA''dhAnasAdhyatvam, tathehA'pi syAditi vAcyam | tatra "AhavanIye juhoti" "gArhapatye havIMSi zrapayati" "dakSiNAgnAvanvAhAryaM pacatI"ti vAkyAntarairhomAdiSu viniyuktAnAM saMskAravizeSaviziSTAgnirUpANAmAhavanIyAdInAM sAdhanAkAGkSAyAmAdhAnavidhAnAt ; teSAM saMskAravizeSAvacchinnatvena rUpeNA'gnipadopAttAnAmAdhAnasAdhyatA yuktA | na ceha vrIhayaH saMskAraviziSTAH kratau viniyuktAH, yenA'vahananavAkye'pi teSAM tadviziSTatayopAdAnAt tena rUpeNA'vahananasAdhyatA yuktA syAt | "vrIhibhiryajete"ti jAtimAtreNa hi vrIhayaH kratau viniyuktAH | na cA'tra saktunyAyena viniyogabhaGgaH kalpayitavyaH, apUrvasAdhanatvalakSaNAmAtreNa pradhAnadvitIyAzrutinirvAhe AkAGkSitadRSTaphalatve, niyamamAtraviSayatayA vidhilAghave ca sambhavati dvitIyAzrutibAdhasyA'nAkAGkSitAdRSTaphalakalpanasyAtyantAprAptiprApakApUrvavidhigauravAbhyupagamasya cA'yuktatvAt | na caivamapUrvasAdhanatvalakSaNAyAmAjyasAnnAyya-juhUnuvAdInAmapyavaghAtaprasaGgaH | vrIhINAM hyavagatApUrvasAdhanatvaM lakSaNIyam | teSAJcA''gneyAdyapUrvasAdhanatvaM taNDulanirvRttyAdidvArakaM "vrIhibhiryajete" tyAdizAstreNA'vagatamiti tallakSaNAyAM yavasAdhAraNyamAtralAbhe'pi kvA'nyatrA'tiprasaGgaH, tathaivA'vazyaM lakSaNIyamavaghAtasya dRSTaphalatvanirvAhAya | tadetatsarvaM "teSAmarthena saMbandhaH" (3|1|6) iti tArtIyAdhikaraNe, "dravya-saMkhyA-hetusamudAyaM vA zrutisaMyogAdi" (9|1|5|11)ti nAvamikAdhikaraNe ca spaSTamiti bhAvaH ||3|| iti tRtIyazlokavyAkhyA caturthazlokasyA'vatArikA evaM prAptAnAmapi rUpabhedAzrayaNenA'prAptimapekSya lakSaNAnugamane vrIhipadalakSitAkArAzrayaNenA'vaghAtaniyamavidhAvativyAptiruktA | idAnIM zakyAkArAzrayaNena anyatra niyamavidhAvativyAptimudAharati- yUpaH sarvo'pyapUrvaH svayamapi vividhAdRSTasaMskArayogAt tasmin daikSe'pi dRSTaM khadiraniyamanaM tatra kA prAptivArtA | tasmAttattatsvarUpe paramanavagataM rUpabhedaM vihAya prAptyaprAptI vicArye kathamapi tadihA'vyAptidoSAnna muktiH ||4|| [p.23] yUpaH prAkRto vaikRtaH sarvo'pi tAvadAhavanIyAdivadalaukikaH; prokSaNajanyAdRSTachedana-takSaNASTAzrIkaraNAdijanyaniyamAdRSTarUpAnekAdRSTasaMskAraviziSTasyaiva yUpazabdArthatvAt | tatra prakRtidravyaniyamavidhiH zrUyate "khAdiro yUpo bhavatI"ti | tatrA'laukikayUpatvAvacchedena khAdiraprAptyabhAvAdapUrvavidhilakSaNasya tatrA'pyativyAptiH syAt | AdhAnadRSTAntena khAdiravidherapUrvavidhitvazaGkA nanvAhavanIyAdyuddezenA''dhAnavidhivadyUpoddezena khAdiravidhirapyapUrvavidhirastu; [p.24] atha paJcamazlokasyA'vatArikA asminneva vikalpe sthalAntare'pyavyAptiM rUpabhedavivakSayA nirasitumazakyAmudAharati-- avyAptiH paurNamAsyAmapi yajanavidhAvatra kalpe prasarpet prAptoddezyasya tena hyadhigataphalatopAMzuyAjasya labhyA | syAtkiJcaiSA''parAgnyAhutiSu phalasamAyojanArthapravRtteH niSpratyUhAgnihotrAhutiyugalamitAdhArasaGkIrtanepi ||5|| darzapUrNamAsaprakaraNe zrUyate "tAvabrUtAvagnISomAvAjyasyaiva nAvupAMzu paurNamAsyAM yajanni"ti | atra vAkye kIrtitAnAM yAga-tadapekSitAgnISomadevatA''jyadravyopAMzutvaguNa-paurNamAsIkAlAnAM madhye kAlavyatiriktAnAM tattadvidhyanuguNazabdAbhAvAdanyataH sambhavatprAptikAnAM teSAM punaHzravaNasya niSphalatvAcca kAlasyaiva vidhiH | na hyagnISomavaktRkavAkyagatasya yajannityasya yAgavidhau; tathApyupAMzuyAjasya "darzapUrNamAsAbhyAM svargakAmo yajete"tyadhikAravAkyoddezyaphalasambandhasiddhyarthaM bhavedeva | etadabhAve vidvadvAkyasthena, adhikAravAkyasthena ca darzapUrNamAsapadena "yadAgneyo'STAkapAlo'mAvAsyAyAM paurNamAsyAJcA'cyuto bhavati" "agnISomIyamekAdazakapAlaM pUrNamAse prAyacchadi"tyutpattivAkyenA'vagatapaurNamAsIkAlayorAgneyAgnISomIyayAgayoreva jhaTiti pravRttinimittAvagatyA prathamagRhItayoH phalasambandhakaraNAdarthAvagamyapaurNamAsIsambandhasyopAMzuyAjasya vilambitapravRttinimittapratItikatayA vidvadvAkyAdhikAravAkyagatapaurNamAsIpadAgRhItasya phalasambandho na sidhyet-iti | bhavadevamatAnuvAdaH yattu-bhavadevenA'sya vidheH prayojanamuktam; yadyantarAlavidhyarthalabhya evopAMzuyAje paurNamAsIkAlaH syAt tadAnIm "Agneyo vai brAhmaNo devatayA, sa someneSTvA'gnISomIyo bhavatI "ti zruteH asomayAjino'gnISomIyapuroDAzAbhAvena tadIyAyAmekapuroDAzAyAM paurNamAsyAM puroDAzadvayAntarAlAbhAvAdupAMzuyAjo na prApnuyAt, prAtisvikakAlavidhisadbhAve tvantarAlamanapekSyaiva paurNamAsyupAMzuyAjAGgamiti siddhestadanusAreNAntarAlazravaNamAgneyottarakAlamAtropalakSaNaM bhavediti ekapuroDAzAyAmapyupAMzuyAjaH prApnoti | atastasyAmupAMzuyAjaprAptyarthaH paurNamAsIvidhiH-iti [p.26] tannirAsaH tadasAram | upalakSaNApAye'pyupalakSyAnapAyanyAyAdevaikapuroDAzAyAmapi tatprAptisambhavena tadarthaM vidhyantarAnapekSaNAt | upalakSaNaM hi puroDAzadvayamupAMzuyAjAGgabhUtasya kAlasya; na tu catasra ityAdivAkyavihitAnAmaSTAnAM homAnAm | dve AhavanIye iti vAkyasattve tu virATsampattivAkyAnusAreNa dazadhA'bhyasanIyasyAgnihotrahomasya dazAnAmabhyAsAnAM madhye dve AhavanIye ityanena parisaMkhyAtAnAmaSTAnAmapekSitamadhikaraNameva catasra ityAdivAkyAbhyAM vidhIyate | tayozcatuHsaMkhyAzravaNantu "samaM syAdazrutatvAt" (10-3-13-53) iti nyAyaprAptAnuvAdakamiti labhyate | evamagnihotravAkya- vihitasyaiva homasya devatAbhedenA'dhikaraNabhedena ca sAyaM prAtazca daza dazA'bhyAsAH; na tadvyatirekeNa homAntarANi santIti paryavasAne'dhikAravAkyagatamagnihotranAma sarvavidhAbhyAsasahitasyA'gnihotrasya samarpakamiti sarvasAdhAraNyena phalasambandhaH prApnoti | yathA "somena yajete"ti vAkyotpannasyaindravAyavAdidevatAbhedenA'bhyasa[p.32]nIyasya somayAgasya jyotiSTomapadaM nAmeti, sarvAbhyAsasahitasya somayAgasyA'dhikAravAkyena phalasambandhaH | na ca "dve AhavanIya" iti vAkyAbhAve'pi virATsampattivAkyamagnihotrahomasya dazadhA'bhyAsavidhAyakamabhyupagamyatAm, tathA sati tatra tvaduktarItyA'STAnAmabhyAsAnAmadhikaraNamAtravidhAyakayorgArhapatyAnvAhAryapacanavAkyayoH saMkhyAzravaNaM nyAyaprAptAnuvAdakaM bhaviSyatItyAparAgnikahomAnAmagnihotrahomAbhyAsarUpatvena phalasambandhaH sidhyediti vAcyam | yadi hyAhavanIyavAkyaM na syAt; [p.32] atha SaSThazlokasyAvataraNam athA'nyatrA'pyavyAptimudAharati-- AkAGkSyAdarthavAdAdupanamati phale karmaNo yadvidhAnam tatrA'pyeSA tadaGgIbhavanakRti bhavetsannidhAnazrutAnAm | yatkiJcidyatra kutrApyanadhikRtavatItyeva lakSmasthitizcet hantyAdyeSu prasiddho niyamavidhirapi syAdapUrvaM vidhAnam ||6|| "vaizvadevIM sAMgrahaNIM nirvaped grAmakAmaH, vaizvadevA vai sajAtAH, vizvAneva devAnsvena bhAgadheyenopadhAvati, ta evA'smai sajAtAn prayacchanti grAmyeva bhavatI"ti zrUyate | tatra sAMgrahaNISTergrAmaphale yadvidhAnam; tanna tasyAstatprAptyartham; rAtrisatranyAyena samanantarapaThitArthavAdAdeva tatprAptisaMbhavAt | AkAGkSyo hyatra vidhinA'rthavAdaH stutilAbhAya | viziSya cA'tra "grAmazabdo'yaM bahvarthaH" iti mahAbhASyokteranekArthasya phalasamarpakasya grAmazabdasya vivakSitArthanirNayalAbhAya | kintu sAMgrahaNyAstadarthavAdAt phalakalpanAyAm "AmanamasyAmanasya devA iti tisra AhutIrjuhoti; etAvanto vai sajAtAH, ye mahAntaH, ye kSullakA yAstriyastAnevAvarundhe; ta enamavaruddhA upatiSThantI"ti tatsannidhipaThitAnAmAmanahomAnAmapi tadarthavAdAtphalArthatvakalpanAprasaktyA teSAM sAMgrahaNyaGgatvaM na prApnuyAditi tatprAptyartham | etatsattve hi pratyakSeNa vidhinA sAMgrahaNyAH zIghraM phalasaMbandhe'vagate phalavadaphalanyAyAdAmanahomAnAM tadaGgatvaM prApnoti | evaJcA'tra vidheyAyAH sAMgrahaNyAH kenA'pi rUpeNoddezyagrAmasaMbandhAprAptirnAstItyatrApyavyAptiranivAryA | nanu lakSaNe prakRtavidhyuddezyamaprApte satIti na vizeSyate; kintu vidhiviSaye anyasminvA prakRtavidhyuddezyam, anyadvA kiJcidaprApte satIti | tathA ca naitAsAmavyAptInAmavakAza iti cet | evaM "vrIhInavahantI"tyAdiH prasiddho niyamavidhirapyapUrvavidhiH syAt | dArzapaurNamAsike prayAjAdau sauryAdivikRtimaprApte sati tadvidhipravRtteH, atastathAvivakSaNaucityAdavyAptiH syAdeveti bhAvaH | iti SaSThazlokavyAkhyA [p.34] atha saptamazlokasyA'vatArikA syAdetat- "vidhiratyantamaprApta" ityasya tadvidhyapravRttAvaprApyaH prasaktimatIti paryavasito'rthastasya zlokasya vyAkhyAnasamaye sUcitaH | tena tasya vidhestatprAptiphalakatvamarthAllabhyate | ato yasminkasmiMzcidyatkiJcidaprApte satIti vivakSAyAmapi tasya tatprAptiphalakatvam ityadhikAMzasya lakSaNe nivezAnnA'vahanananiyamavidhAvativyAptiprasaGgaH; tasya sauryAdiSu prayAjAdiprAptiphalakatvAbhAvAt | evaJca vaikRtAgnyatigrAhyavidhiSvavidheyAnAmapi guNakAmAnAM tattadvidhyuddezyAsu vikRtiSu prApteH; dvAdazAhikagAvAmayanikatryanIkAntargata-tadanantargatavidhiSu vidheyAnAmevA'gratAnAM tattadvidhyanuddezyAsu dvAdazAhavikRtiSu prApteH | "upAMzu paurNamAsyAM yajanni"ti vidhau avidheyasyopAMzuyAjasya tadvidhyanuddezye svarge prApteH; "dve AhavanIya" iti vidhAvavidheyAnAmAparAgnikahomAnAmanuddezye svarge prApteH, sAMgrahaNIphalavidhAvavidheyAnAM tatsannidhipaThitAnAmanuddezye tadvidheye karmaNi prAptezca phalasya sadbhAvAnna kvA'pyavyAptizaGkAvakAzo'pItyAzaGkyA''ha- uddezye'nyatra vA yadvidhipadamitaradvA'pi na prAptimatsyAt tatprAptiM tatra tanvanvidhiriti karaNe'pyevamavyAptireva | jyotiSTomAdikAnAM tridivagatiphale sarvakAmoktisiddhe kiM prApyaM dharmajAte prakaraNagamite svargakAmazrutibhyAm ||7|| "jyotiSTomena svargakAmo yajeta" "darza-pUrNamAsAbhyAM svargakAmo yajete"ti zrUyate | idaM vAkyadvayaM jyotiSTomazabdagRhItasya somayAgasya, darzapUrNamAsazabdagRhItAnAmAgneyAdiyAgAnAJca na svarUpaprAptyartham; "somena yajete"ti vAkyena, AgneyAdivAkyaizca teSAM prAptatvAditi tAvatspaSTameva | nA'pi teSAM svargaphalasaMbandhaprAptyartham | "ekasmai vA anye yajJakratavaH kAmAyA''hriyante, sarvebhyo jyotiSTomaH" " ekasmai vA anye yajJakratavaH kAmAyA''hriyante sarvebhyaH kAmebhyo darzapUrNamAsAvi"ti vacanAbhyAM teSAM pazvAdiphalakatvavatsvargaphalakatvasyApi prAptisambhavAt | nA'pyaGgasaMbandhabodhanArtham; prakaraNakalpyena vAkyena tatprApti[p.35]sambhavAt | na hi svargakAmavAkyayoraGgasaMbandhabodhakatvamapi taducitazabdakalpanAM vinA sambhavati; tayoraGgasamarpakazabdAbhAvAt | na ca dIkSaNIyAdisamidAdivAkyavizeSitayostatsaMbandhabodhakatvaM sambhavatIti vAcyam | dIkSaNIyAdivAkyeSvapi yacchabdopabandharahitasvatantrAkhyAtasattvena svatasteSAmanyavizeSaNatvAlAbhAt | tasmAtsvargakAmavAkyayoraGgasaMbandhabodhakatvAGgIkAre'pi tayordIkSaNIyAdisamidAdivAkyAnAM cA'pratyakSamapyekavAkyatvaM prakaraNabalenaikavAkyatApAdakazabdakalpanayopapAdanIyam | tathA hi- jyotiSTomena, darza-pUrNamAsAbhyAM vA svargApUrvaM bhAvayedityukte bhavati prakArAkAGkSA kathaM tena, tAbhyAM vA tadbhAvanIyamiti; anirbhAtaprakAratvAt | na hi "godhUmairmaNDakAH kAryA' ityukte tatprakArAnabhijJasya niyojyasyA''kAGkSA na bhavati kathaM godhUmairmaNDakAH kartavyA iti; "kuThAreNa vRkSaM chindhI"tyukte tatprakArAnabhijJasya bhavatyeva tadAkAGkSA, taM prati hastAbhyAM kuThAraM gRhItvA tadudyamana-nipAtanavyApArAvarttaneneti vaktavyameva | etatprakArasya lokaprasiddhataratvAtprAyeNa tatra na bhavatyAkAGkSA; iha tu jyotiSTomAdibhiH svargApUrvasampAdanasyA'tyantAlaukikatvAtprakArAkAGkSA avazyambhAvinI | tathA dIkSaNIyAdInAmapyasti prayojanAkAGkSA| tadiha parasparAkAGkSAlakSaNe prakaraNe sati "revatISu vAravantIyamagniSTomasAma kRtvA pazukAmo hyetena yajete"tivat dIkSaNIyAdivAkyAnAM pratyakSe jyotiSTomAdivAkyaikavAkyatve cA'sati, yena zabdena dIkSaNIyAdivyApArANAM jyotiSTomAdivizeSaNatocyate, tAdRkzabdakalpanenA'pratyakSamekavAkyatvaM prakaraNabalena kalpanIyam | yathA "kIdRzaM devadattagRhami"ti prakArAkAGkSAyAM dvAropAntalikhitazaGkhapadmanidhi bahucampakAlaGkRtaniSkuTaM devadattagRhamityabhidhAnato vizeSaNamanuktvA; iha devadattagRhaM varttate, dvAropAnte likhite zaGkhapadme staH, niSkuTe campakAH santItyetAvatyukte'pi gRhasya prakArAkAGkSayA, prakAropadezasya prayojanAkAGkSayA ca taducitazabdakalpanayaikavAkyatvaM kalpyata ityevamevopapAditaM tantraratne | tathA ca svargakAmavAkyayoraGgasaMbandhabodhakatvAnaGgIkAre'pi prakaraNabalAttadbodhakaM vAkyaM kalpayituM zakyam | [p.36] yadyapi sarvakAmavAkyayorAkhyAtarahitayoryAgabhAvanA na zrUyate; tathA'pi caturthyA jyotiSTomasya, darza-pUrNamAsayozca sarvakAmArthatvapratipAdanAt jyotiSTomena, darzapUrNamAsAbhyAJca svarga-pazu-putra-hiraNyAdyapUrvaM bhAvayedityarthato lAbhAtsyAdeva tatprakArAkAGkSA, dIkSaNIyAdInAm, samidAdInAJca pUrvavatprayojanAkAGkSA sthitaiva | evamubhayAkAGkSAlakSaNena prakaraNena dIkSaNIyAdibhiH samidAdibhizcopakRtya, jyotiSTomena, darzapUrNamAsAbhyAJca svarga-pazvAdyapUrvaM bhAvayediti vAkyaM kimiti kalpayituM na zakyam | evaM svargasaMbandhasyA'GgasaMbandhasya cA'nyalabhyatve'pi kecana svargasaMbandhe sarvakAmavacanalabhye svargakAmavAkyayorakRtArthatvaM manyamAnAstayoraGgasaMbandhabodhanArthatvamityAhuH | anye tu- sarvakAmazrutyavirodhena svargakAmavAkyayoreva svargasaMbandhabodhanArthatvam; sarvakAmavAkyayostu sAmAnyamukhapravRttayoH svargetarasaMbandhabodhanArthatvamityAhuH ubhayorapyanyataH prAptisambhavAttadvyatirekeNa ca kvacitkasya citprApaNIyasyA'darzanAt svargakAmavAkyayoruktalakSaNAvyAptiH sarvathaivA'nivAryeti bhAvaH | iti saptamazlokavyAkhyA athASTamazlokasyA'vataraNam atha prakArAntareNodAhRteSu, taditarApUrvavidhiSu cA'vyAptimudAharati-- nAnAzAkhAgnihotraprabhRtividhiSu cA'vyAptirevaM sati syAt tattadvidhyapravRttAvapi tadadhigatiH syAddhi vidhyantareNa | sA sA prAptistu naivA'bhyudayaphalavatItyevamaprAptisattvam kRtvA'vyApternirAse na niyama-parisaMkhyAnuvRtternivRttiH ||8|| [p.37] agnihotrAdividhayastAvannAnAzAkhAsu zrUyante | tena tattacchAkhAmnAtavidhyabhAve nAnAzAkhAsu viprakIrNAnAmaGgAnAmivA'gnihotrAdInAmapi zAkhAntarataH prAptiH syAdeva | tatazca tattadvidhyabhAve'nyataH prAptyasambhavarUpasyA'prAptavizeSaNArthasya abhAvAtsarveSvapi nAnAzAkhAmnAtApUrvavidhiSvavyAptiranivAryA | zaGkA-nirAsau nanu zAkhAntarAdhikaraNe teSAM tattacchAkhAdhyAyinaM prati svasvazAkhAdhyayanaprAthamyenA'jJAtajJApakatvaM samarthitam | satyam | na tvajJAtajJApakatvasiddhimAtreNa aprAptavizeSaNArtho vivakSito labhyate | na cA'jJAtatvamevA'prAptazabdArthaH | tathA sati niyama-parisaMkhyayorapi arthAkSepamantraliGgAbhyAM prAgeva pravRttayorajJAtajJApakatvasattvena tayorativyAptyApatteH | nanvabhyudayaziraskaprAptisambhavarAhityamaprApta ityanena vivakSitam | tatazca "svAdhyAyo'dhyetavya" iti niyamavidhibalAdadhyayanagRhItasya svazAkhAntargatavAkyajanyatadarthajJAnasyaivA'bhyudayakAritvena svazAkhAntargatavAkyasadbhAve zAkhAntaragatavAkyajanyajJAnasyA'bhyudayakAritvasyA'bhAvAttattacchAkhAdhyAyipuruSApekSayA sarveSAmagnihotrAdividhInAmajJAtajJApakatvavat, aprAptaprAptiphalakatvamapi vivakSitarUpamastyeveti, na nAnAzAkhAgatAgnihotrAdividhiSvavyAptiH | nA'pi svargakAmavAkyeSvavyAptiH; teSvapi sAptadazyavAkyanyAyenA'bhyudayaphalakaprAptyabhAvasattvAt | tathA hi- "saptadaza sAmidhenIranubrUyAdi"tyanArabhya zrUyate | atra vidhIyamAnasya sAptadazyasya na sAmidhenIdvArA prakRtau nivezaH; prAkaraNikatatpAJcadazyAvaruddhatvAt, kintu vikRtiSveva | tatrA'pi zrutasAptadazyAsu mitravindAdiSveva nivezaH; na sarvAsu vikRtiSu | anArabhyazrutaM hi sAptadazyaM yAvatsAmidhenIdvArA sakalavikRtyaGgaM kalpyate; tAvatprAkaraNikairvAkyairmitravindAdisaMbandhe kRte kSINA'rthApattirna kratvantarasaMbandhaM kalpayati | tenA'nArabhyAdhItasya sAptadazyasyopasaMhArArthAni tattatprakaraNAdhItAni sAptadazyavacanAni | tatazca prAkaraNikavacanaiH sAptadazyasya kratusaMbandhasiddhiH, anArabhyAdhItavAkyena sAmidhenIsaMbandhasiddhiriti ca [p.38] mImAMsakamaryAdA atrA'nArabhyavidhAnAbhAve'pi sAptadazyasya kratusaMbandhavatsAmidhenIsaMbandho'pi prAkaraNikavacanaireva siddhyatIti; anArabhyavidhAnasya vaiyarthyazaGkA prApnoti | atroktaM tantraratne- "yadyapi prAkaraNikenaiva kratusaMbandhaH, sAmidhenIsaMbandhazca sidhyati | tathA'pi vAkyadvayAmnAnAt evaM vijJAnamabhyudayakArIti vijJAyate; arthavAdaprarocitavat" iti | tatazcA'sya nyAyasya sArvakAmyavAkyaprAptArthakasvargakAmavAkyasAdhAraNyAttatrA'pyabhyudayaziraskaprAptyabhAvo vaktuM zakyata iti cet tathA sati niyama-parisaMkhyAvidhyoratyantamaprApta iti vizeSaNAbhyAM nivRttirna syAt | tatrA'pi svAdhyAyaniyamavidhibalAdevA'rthAkSepamantraliGgAbhyAmavaghAtAdiprApterabhyudayakAritvAbhAvasya vaktuM zakyatvAt | tasmAdanyataH prAptyabhAvamAtramihA'prAptiriti vaktavyam, ityudAhRtasthaleSvavyAptiH sudRDhaiveti bhAvaH ||8|| ityaSTamazlokavyAkhyA

[p.70] ekAdazazlokasyAvatArikA aprApta iti satisaptamIpakSe parisaMkhyAvyAvRttirna bhavitumarhatIti nirUpaNam | kiJcAprApta ityasya satisaptamIpakSe parisaMkhyAnivartakatvaM na labhyata iti dUSaNAntaramAha- kiJcA'prApte satIti prabhavati parisaMkhyAnivRttiM na kartuM nityAprAptAM nivRttiM ghaTayati khalu sA'pyatra ko vA vizeSaH | bhAvAprAptiniSedhAtmakavidhinikaravyApakAvyAptiduSTA satyapyevaM niSedhazrutimukhaparisaMkhyAsu cA'tiprasaktiH ||11|| aprApte satItivizeSaNaM parisaMkhyAnivRttiM kartuM na prabhavati | yataH parisaMkhyApyatyantamaprAptAmitaranivRttiM prApayatyeva | tathA ca "imAmagRbhNan razanAmRtasyetyazvAbhidhAnImAdatta" iti parisaMkhyAvidhiH prAptaviSayo'pi gardabharazanAto nivRttau mantramaprAptAyAM satyAM tatprAptiphalako bhavatIti kathaM viSayasaptamIpakSa iva satisaptamIpakSe tadvyAvRttiraprAptivizeSaNena labhyate | na hyapUrvavidhitaH parisaMkhyAvidheraprAptaprAptiphalakatve'sti kazcidvizeSaH | nanvayamasti vizeSaH, yatparisaMkhyAvidhau nivRttirUpasyA'bhAvasyAprAptasya prAptiH phalam, apUrvavidhau tu bhAvasyeti bhAvAprAptireva cehA'prAptirityanena vivakSitA| loke prAptizabdasya nivRttipratibhaTavAcitvena prasiddhatayA bhAvazabdAprayoge'pi tadviSayatvalAbhAditi cet, evaM sati niSedhAtmakeSu "dIkSito na juhoti, na tau pazau karoti" "nAtirAtre SoDazinaM gRhNAti" ityAdisarveSvapyapUrvavidhiSvavyAptiH syAt teSAM dIkSAmadhyapazvatirAtrAdyaprAptapuruSArthahomAjyabhAgaSoDazinivRttyAdirUpAbhAvaprAptiphalaka[p.71]tvAt | na ca niSedhA vidhayo na bhavantIti vAcyam | dArzapUrNamAsike "nAnRtaM vadedi"ti niSedhe sUtrakRtaiva vidhitvasya vyavasthApitatvAt | nanvanuvAdapratiyogividhitvaM tatra tasya vyavasthApitam | tatra hi tasya darzapUrNamAsaprakaraNe zrutasya "nAnRtaM vadedi"tiniSedhasya smRtidRSTapuruSArthaniSedhAnuvAdakatvamAzaGkya kratvaGgatvenA'nRtavadanavarjanavidhirayam; tena kratumadhye'nRtavadane puruSArthaniSedhAtikramAt pratyavAyavat, kratvarthaniSedhAtikramAt kratuvaiguNyaM ca bhavatItyuktam | iha tu niSedhapratiyogividhitvaM lakSyarUpam | ato na tadavyApanaM doSa iti cet | maivam| ihApyanuvAdapratiyogividhereva lakSyatvAt | apUrvavidhyAdyudAharaNeSu vidhitvasamarthanAya prApyaprAptivivecanavat, bhAvAbhAvavivecanA'darzanAt | bhavatu vA vidhimukhapravRttApUrvavidhInAmevA'tra lakSyatvAGgIkAreNa kathaJcidavyAptiparihAraH; tathApyuktavivakSAyAM niSedhamukhapravRttaparisaMkhyAtivyAptiranivAryA | tatrA'prAptatvena prApaNIyAyA itaranivRtterbhAvarUpatvAt | yathA "zaMyvantA prAyaNIyA santiSThate, na patnIH saMyAjayantI"ti | atra prAyaNIyAyAH zaMyvantasamApanavidhAnAttata udIcyAnAM sarveSAmaGgAnAmakaraNe prApte, na patnIH saMyAjayantIti vacanaM patnIsaMyAjAneva na kuryAt; aGgAntarANi tu kuryAditi parisaMkhyArUpeNa prAyaNIyAyAmaGgAntaraprAptyartham | yathA vA zaGkhasmRtau taijasadravyazuddhiprakaraNe "sUtikocchiSTabhANDasya surAmadyahatasya ca | triH saptamArjanAcchuddhirna tu kAMsyasya tApanam ||" ityatra mArjanavattApanasya vidhAnAbhAvAtsarveSAM taijasAnAM tApanAkaraNe prApte na tu kAMsyasyeti vacanaM kAMsyasyaiva tApanaM na kAryamanyasya tu kAryamiti parisaMkhyArUpeNa taijasAntarANAM tApanaprAptyartham | tathaiva vyAkhyAtamaparArkeNa "taijasAnAM sUtikocchiSTAdike svalpopaghAte yavacUDAdibhirekaviMzatikRtvo mArjanAcchuddhirna tu kAMsyasya tApanam, arthAtkAMsyavyatiriktasya tApanamapi kAryamiti gamyate" iti | yadyapyatrA''dyodAharaNe vastutaH parisaMkhyA neSyate, zaMyvantasaMsthAyAH parisaMkhyAyAzca vidhidvayena vAkyabhedasya tulyabalavidhidvayaprAptayoH zaMyvantasaMsthA [p.72] patnIsaMyAjavarjaM kRtsnasaMsthayorvikalpasya prAptaviSayatvena tridoSAyAH parisaMkhyAyAzca parihArAya na patnIH saMyAjayantIti zaMyvantasaMsthAvidherarthavAdaH patnIsaMyAjAnanuSThAnalAghavena tatprazaMsArtha iti zaMyviDAntatve vikalpaH syAditi dAzamikAdhikaraNe nirNItatvAt | tathA'pi pUrvapakSarItyA tadudAharaNam | yatraivaM vidhyantarazeSatvaM na sambhavati tatra tadadhikaraNapUrvapakSarItyA parisaMkhyaivAvatiSThata iti tathAbhUtamudAharaNamihAnveSaNIyam | prasiddhA cAnyatrApi "na nIlIvasanaM dhAryaM na bhUpAlairanutsave" ityAdiSu smRtiSu; yajJadattadevadatta-viSNumitrA na bhojanIyAH, utsaveSvapi viSNumitro na bhojanIya ityAdilokavyavahAreSu ca vizeSaniSedhaH zeSAbhyanujJApara iti niSedhamukhaparisaMkhyA | atastatrAtivyAptiH syAdevetibhAvaH | ityekAdazazlokavyAkhyA | apUrvavidhilakSaNaghaTakAprAptavizeSaNavicAradvArA doSAnudbhAvyAtyantavizeSaNArthavicAradvAreNa dvAdazazlokasyopakramaH | evamaprAptavizeSaNavicAramukhena lakSaNasya doSA darzitAH | athA'tyantavizeSaNArthavicAramukhenA'pi tasya doSAndarzayati | yenA'tivyAptibhaGgaH kila niyamavidhau kiM tadAtyantikatvaM sarvoddezyAnuvRttiryadi punaruditAvyAptayaH syurvikalpe | prAptoddezyaikadezA api khalu kuhaciccAritArthyaM bhajantaH zrUyante sArvakAmyaprabhRtiSu vidhayasteSu cA'vyAptayaH syuH ||12|| AtyantikatvaM dvedhA vikalpya satisaptamI-viSayasaptamIpakSayoH sambhAvitAnAM doSANAM nirdezaH | [p.73] yena vizeSaNena niyamavidhau lakSaNasyA'tivyAptinirAso'bhimataH; tadaprApterAtyantikatvaM kiM nAma ? yadyucyate sarvoddezyAnuvRttiraprApterAtyantikatvam | avaghAtasya ca na sarveSu vrIhiSvaprAptiH; keSuciddarzapUrNamAsaprayogagateSu vrIhiSvavaghAtasya svata eva prApteH anyeSu vidalanaprAptiH, teSveva tadaprAptisattvAditi | evaM tarhi prathamazlokodbhAvitA'vyAptayaH prAk samAhitAH punarapi vikalpamukhenonmajjeyuH| tathA hi-sarvoddezyAnuvRttirityatroddezyapadena prApaNIyoddezyaM vivakSitam; prAkRtavidhyuddezyaM vA? Adye viSayasaptamIpakSodbhAvitavaikRtAgnayAdividhyavyApteH, dvitIye satisaptamIpakSodbhAvitAgratAvidhyavyAptezca punarunmajjanApAtaH| "zyenacitaM cinvIta svargakAma" ityAdivAkyaiH svasvoddezyeSu svargAdiSu zyenAkRtyAdiguNAnAM prAptau satyAmeva svargAdyuddezena teSAmanuSThAnam| vaikRtaprayogamadhye paraM na prAptamiti, vikRtiSvagnayAdipunarvidhInAM tatprApaNaM phalamityupeyate, evamagratAvidhyuddezyeSu dvAdazAhikagAvAmayanikeSu AdyadvitIyeSvahassu prAptAnAmevAgratAnAM tadvikRtiSu prAptirvidhiphalamityupeyate| tatra vikRtiSu prApaNIyAnAM zyenAdiguNAnAM svasvoddezyeSu svargAdiSu agratAnAM ca vidhyanvitoddezye dvAdazAhikagAvAmayanikeSvahaHsu kvAcitkyapyaprAptirnAsti kathaM sA sArvatrikI samarthyeta | api coddezyaikadeze prAptimanto'pi tadekadezAntare caritArthA vidhayo dRzyante yathA "ekasmai vA anye yajJakratavaH kAmAyA''hriyante sarvebhyo jyotiSTomaH, sarvebhyo darzapUrNamAsAvi"ti | atra jyotiSToma-darzapUrNamAsAnAM svargArthatvaM prAtisvikavidhinA prAptamiti phalAntareSveva vidhicAritArthyam | abhyudayeSTivAkyasya bhASyaTupTIkAvalambanena pUrvadevatApanayaH, vakSyamANadevatAsambandha ityarthadvayasya pratipAdanam | yathA vAbhyudayeSTyAM "taNDulAnvibhaje"diti taNDuladadhizRtAnAM pUrvadevatApanayaH, vakSyamANadevatAbhyo vibhAgo vA madhyamAdivAkyaisteSAM devatAntaravidhAnenaiva [p.74] prApta iti; upAMzuyAjAjya eva vidhicAritArthyam | tathA hi "vi vA etaM prajayA pazubhirardhayati, vardhayatyasya bhrAtRvyaM yasya havirniruptaM purastAccandramA abhyudeti sa tredhA taNDulAnvibhajet, ye madhyamAstAnagnaye dAtre puroDAzamaSTAkapAlaM nirvapedye sthaviSThAstAnindrAya pradAtre dadhnazcaruM ye'NiSThAstAnviSNave zipiviSTAya zRte carumi"ti zrUyate | atra caturdazyAmamAvAsyAbhrAntyA darzopakramaM kRtavatastatprAyazcittavidhAne; tredhA taNDulAnAM madhyamAdibhAvena tredhA vibhAgo na vidhIyate; ye madhyamA ityAdivAkyaistatsiddheH, kintu sarveSAM dArzikahaviSAM pUrvadevatAbhyo'panayo vidhIyate | vibhajedityukte hyayamartho'vagamyate yAni saMyuktAni tAni vibhajediti | taNDulAdIni havIMSi ca devatAbhiH saMyuktAni | atastaNDulazabdo vibhAgakarmatvenA'rthaprAptadArzikahavirmAtropalakSaka iti teSAM pUrvadevatApanayo vidhIyata iti yuktam | athavA "manuH putrebhyo dAyaM vyabhajadi"ti (tai0 saM0 kAH 3.1.1.) prayogadarzanAdiha dArzikahaviSAM vakSyamANadAtRtvAdiguNakAgnayAdidevatAbhyo vibhajanaM vidhIyate, pUrvadevatApanayastvarthasiddha ityevamasya vAkyasyArthadvayaM bhASyaTupTIkayordarzitam | tatrA'rthadvaye'pi taNDula-dadhizRteSu na vidhezcAritArthyaM devatAntarasaMyojanena pUrvadevatApanayasya; vizeSato vibhajanena sAmAnyavibhAgasyApyarthaprAptatvAt | kintu taNDulazabdena dArzikahavirmAtropalakSakena lakSyamANa upAMzuyAjAjye tasya cAritArthyam | upAMzuyAjAjyasyA'gre devatAntarasaMyojanAbhAvena pUrvadevatApanayasya sAmAnyavibhajanasya cA'rthaprAptyabhAvAt | ata eva pUrvadevatApanayapakSe upAMzuyAjAjyasya pUrvadevatAyA apanayAt devatAntarasya cA'vidhAnAt devatAmantareNa yaSTumazakyatvAdupAMzuyAjasya lopaH | sAmAnyavibhajanapakSe upAMzuyAjAjyasyA'pi sAmAnyena vakSyamANadevatAbhyo nirdiSTatvAdagre devatAntaravizeSasya cA'nirdezAdvakSyamANAbhyaH sarvAbhyo devatAbhyo vibhajya dAtavyamiti bhASyaTupTIkayoH "upAMzuyAje vacanAdyathAprakRti" (6|5|2|10) ityetadadhikaraNe bahRcabrAhmaNAnusAreNAmAvAsyAyAmupAMzuyAjasattAmabhyupetya nirUpitam | pUrvadevatApanayasAmAnyavibhAgayorArthikatvasiddhizaGkA | nanu taNDula-zRta-sAnnAyyeSvapi pUrvadevatApanayasAmAnyavibhAgau nArthasiddhau; [p.75] atha trayodazazlokasya vyAkhyA syAdetat-sarvoddezyAnuvRttiriha na sarvAsUddezyavyaktiSvaprApteranuvRttirvivakSitA, kiMtvekasyAmapyuddezyavyaktau sarvAtmanAnuvRttistadvyApitvarUpA | evaM ca sArvakAmyavidhyAdiSu nAvyAptiH; teSu svargAtiriktaphalopAMzuyAjadravyAdirUpakiJcitkiJciduddezyavyApakAprApteH sattvAt | nA'pi niyamavyAvRttyalAbhaH, niyamavidhau kaciduddezye vyApikAyA aprApterabhAvAt | sarveSvapi dArzapUrNamAsikeSu vrIhiSvAkSepAdavahananasya pAkSikaprAptisattvAt | na hi "vrIhInavahantI"ti niyamavidhyabhAve keSucidvrIhiSvavahananam; keSucid vidalanAdikaM ca vyavasthitamAkSepataH prApyate, yena kvacidapyuddezye tadaprAptirvyApikA syAt; kintu sarveSvapi vrIhiSvavahanena vA vaituSyaM kAryaM vidalanAdinA veti tadubhayamapi pakSe prApyate; na caikatrobhayAnuSThAnasya viruddhatvAdubhayoH prAptirnAstIti zaGkanIyam | nahi prAptirnAmAnuSThAnameva; tathA sati [p.76] kvaciddarzapUrNamAsaprayoge vidalanasyA'pyanuSThAne niyamavidhivirodhApatteH | kintu niyamavidhyabhAve lokasiddhavaituSyakAraNatvAttadarthamavahananam; vidalanAdikaM vA'nuSThIyetetyevamanuSThAnatarkaNamAtraM prAptiH, taccaikatrA'pi na viruddham | na hyavirodhe'pi niyamavidhyabhAve kvacitprayoge'vahananam; prayogAntare vidalanAdikaM cAnuSThIyetetyevaM tarkaH pravarttata iti niyantuM zakyate | kaMcitprayogavizeSamevA'dhikRtya niyamazAstrAbhAve ihA'vahananaM vidalanAdikaM vAnuSThIyetetyevamapi tarkapravRttisambhavAt | tasmAduktavivakSAyAM na kazciddoSa ityata Aha- uddezyavyApikA sA yadi bhavati tadA''pyAdimAvyAptibandhaH syAdavyAptizca gAvAmayanikaSaDahe pRSThasAmnorvidhAne | anyArthaprApaNodyanniyama-haraNayorapyativyAptirittham taccet traikAlikatvaM na tu vidhirapavAdApavAdastathA syAt ||13|| evaM vivakSAyAmapi prathamazlokoktA'gnividhyAdyavyAptipunarunmajjanarUpaH prAguktadoSaH syAdeva; tadudAharaNeSu kvacidapi tattaduddezye'prApterabhAvAt | anyatra cA'vyAptiradhikA syAt | tathA hi- dvAdazAhe pRSThyaH SaDaha utpannaH | sa ca krameNa rathantarabRhadvairUpavairAjazAkvararaivatAkhyapRSThastotrasAdhanasAmavatAM SaNNAmahnAM samAhAraH | gavAmayane punaH zrUyate "pRSThyaH SaDaho bRhadrathantarasAmA kAryaH" iti | tena gAvAmayanikaSaDahasya bRhadrathantare eva pRSThasAmanI iti vairUpAdIni nivarttante | tatazca SaDahoddezena bRhadrathantaravidhau AdyasyAhno rathantarasAmatvam, dvitIyasya bRhatsAmatvaM ca dvAdazAhikaSaDahe klRptamihA'pi prAptamityuddezyavyApakAprApterabhAvAdavyAptiH | bRhadrathantarasAmnoH samuccayavidhAnamiti zaMkA | nanvihaikaikasyAhno bRhadrathantarasAmatvaM vidhIyate | "saMsava ubhe kuryAt" "gosava ubhe kuryAdi"tyAdau yathA | tattu kvacidapyahani na prAptamiti cet na | prakRtau bRhadrathantarayoH parasparanairapekSyeNa pRSThastotrasAdhanatvAttathaiva prAkRtanairapekSyA'bAdhena kvacit bRhat, kvacidrathantaramiti vidhAnopapattau tadbAdhanAyogAt | na [p.77] ca vAcyaM bRhacca rathantaraM ca bRhadrathantare, bRhadrathantare sAmanI asyeti bRhadrathantarasAmeti dvandvagarbho'tra bahuvrIhiH; tatazca dvandvAvagatasAhityabalAdekaikasyAhna ubhayasAmatvaM vidheyamiti akRtvaiva dvandvasamAsaM bRhatsAmA'sya; rathantaraM sAmAsyetyanekapadabahuvrIhisvIkArasambhavAt | na ca "dvandva-tatpuruSayoruttarapade nityasamAsavacanami"ti kAtyAyanavacanAdiha dvandvasamAso niyata iti vAcyam | yatra dvandvatatpuruSayoH satoranyacchabdarUpam; asatozcAnyadbhavati; tatra samAsakRtarUpaniyamArthaM tadvArtikapravRtteH | yathA-vAktvacapriyaH, paJcagavadhanaH iti | atra hi dvandva-tatpuruSayorakaraNe "dvandvAccudaSahAntAtsamAhAre" iti "gorataddhitalukI"ti samAsAntapratyayayoraprAptervAktvakpriyaH paJcagodhana itirUpaM sidhyet | iha tu dvedhA'pi rUpasyAbhedAnnyAyato'rthe nirNetavye pratyekaprApakacodakAnugrahAyA'nekapadabahuvrIhyAzrayaNameva yuktam | yadyapi dvandvagarbha evA'tra bahuvrIhiH syAt; tathApi prakRtitaH prAptaM nairapekSyaM na bAdhitavyam | SaDahApekSayA sAhityasyA'rthaprAptasya tadviSayatvopapatteH | anyathA bRhadrathantarayoH SaDahasaMbandhasya, tayorekaikasminnevA'hani sAhityasya ca vidheyatve bRhadrathantare evA'sya SaDahasya sAmanI te caikaikasminnahani parasparasahite karttavye ityanekArthavidhAnena vAkyabhedaprasaGgAt | na hyatra saMsavAdivaccodakaprAptayorbRhadrathantarayoH sAhityamAtravidhAnaM zakyam, tRtIyAdiSvahaHsu tayorapi prApaNIyatvAt | tasmAdiha nobhayasAmatvavidhAnam | tathaiva vyavasthApitaM "pRSThasya yugapadvidherekAhavadvisAmatvami"ti dAzamikAdhikaraNe| nanvastvekaikasyAhna ekaikasAmavidhAnaM tathA'pi bRhadrathantarasAmeti pAThakramAdAdyasyA'hno bRhat; alpActarapUrvanirdezArthatayA'nyathAsiddhena bRhadrathantarapAThakrameNa prakRtiprAptabAdhanAyogAt | "jagatI vai chandAMsi pratyavarohanti, AgrayaNaM grahAH, bRhatpRSThAnI"ti gavAmayanasthottare pakSasi SADahikasya paJcamasyA'hno bRhatpRSThatvaliGgenA''dyasya rathantaraM dvitIyasya bRhadityAdikramasyaiva nirNayAcca | bRhadrathantarasAmA kArya ityAdividhayo hi gavAmayanasya pUrvasminpakSasyavAntaraprakaraNena nivizante-pUrvasminpakSasyanuSThitAzca chandomatryanIkapRSThasAmastomagaNA uttare pakSasi [p.78] prAtilomyenA'nuSTheyAH | "pratyavarohiNo mAsA bhavanti uttare pakSasyAvRttA gaNAH" ityAdidarzanAt | tatazca yadi pUrvasminpakSasyayugmasyA'hno rathantaraM, yugmasya bRhaditi niyamaH syAttadaivottare pakSasyayugmasya paJcamasyA'hno bRhatpRSThatAdarzanamupapadyate | tasmAt bRhadrathantarasAmA kArya ityatrAdyasyA'hno rathantaraM dvitIyasya bRhadityAdikrameNaiva SaDahoddezena bRhadrathantarasAmatvaM vidhIyate; tasya ca SaDaharUpoddezyavyApikA'prAptirnAstIti avyAptiranivAryA | api ca yairvidhibhiH svoddezye vidheyasyA'prAptasya prApaNaM kriyamANamapi tatrA'pravRttivizeSakaratvAdanyArthatvaM vAcyamiti, tena kasya citprApaNaM niyamanaM parivarjanaM vA samudeti; teSAM navamazlokAvatArikodAhRtAnAM madhye yAvanyArthaprApaNodyanniyamaparivarjanavidhI "sAmnA stuvIta" "yadi rathantarasAmA somaH syAdi"ti, tayorvidhyorativyAptirapyevaM syAt | viSayazuddhyarthamativyAptisthalaM niyamavidherudAharaNAntaram | viSayazuddhyarthamanyadapi niyamavidhirUpamativyAptisthalamudAhriyate | "prajApatirvA idameka AsIdi"tyAdinA prajApatisRSTAgnivAyyAdityotpannatvena vedAnAM prazaMsayopakramya zrUyate tasmAd "uccairRcA kriyate, upAMzu yajuSA, uccaiH sAmne"ti | atropakrame vedAnAM prazaMsayA vedaviSaye kiJcidvidhAsyata ityavagamAt; tadanurodhena RgAdizabdA RgAdiprAyaRgvedAdiparAH | tathA ca Rgvedena yat kriyate-vidhIyate taduccairityAdiprakAreNa RgvedAdividheyakarmoddezenoccaistvAdividhaya iti sthitamupakramAdhikaraNe | karmasu ca sAkSAnnivezamuccaistvAdayo na labhante | ataste tadaGgamantreSvavataranta RgvedAdividheyakarmAGgamantrANAmaniyatasvaraprAptau svaravizeSaniyamArthA ete vidhayaH paryavasyanti | tatazcaiteSu vidhiSu vidheyAnAmuccaistvAdInAM pArSNikAnvayaviSayeSu maMtreSu pAkSikaprAptisattve'pyuddezyeSu karmasu kathamapi prAptyabhAvAtteSvapi lakSaNasyA'tivyAptiH syAt | nanu karmoddezenoccaistvAdividhayo'prAptaviSayatvAdapUrvAdividhaya eva yuktAH, kathamativyAptiriti cet | ucyate-yatra yadanuSThAnasya yasya vidheH phalam, tatra tadanuSThAnaprAptyaprAptivivecanenaiva niyamApUrvavidhitvavyavasthA kAryA; natu yatra yaccheSatvaM [p.79] vidhibodhyam tatra taccheSatvaprAptyaprAptivivecanena | tathA satyavaghAtagatApUrvIyatvazeSatvAprAptyA tadvidherapyapUrvavidhitvaprasaGgAt | na hi tatroddezyatAvacchedakAvacchedenAprAptiH ihoddezyasvarUpa evA'prAptirityetAvatA kazcidvizeSo'sti, vivakSitarUpabhedenA'tyantAprApterubhayatrApyaviziSTatvAt | na ca kenA'pi rUpeNa prAptyabhAva evA'tyantAprAptiH, svargAdigatayAgakAryatvAderapi sAmAnyatodRSTAnumAnAtprAptisambhavena tatrA'pUrvavidhyabhAvApatteH | tatazca yathApUrvIyoddezenAvaghAtavidhAne'pi vrIhiSvavaghAtAnuSThAnameva vidheH phalam; tattu pakSe prApyata iti niyamavidhirAzritaH | evamiha RgvedAdividheyakarmoddezena svaravizeSavidhAne'pi tadaGgamantreSu svaravizeSAnuSThAnameva vidheH phalam; tat pakSe prApyata iti niyamavidhirevAzrayituM yuktaH | anenaiva nyAyena pUrvodAhRtavidhyorapi niyama-parisaMkhyAtvamupapAdanIyam | bhASyamatena guNamukhyavyatikramAdhikaraNasya varNamukhena svaravizeSe niyamavidhyabhAvazaGkA | nanu "guNa-mukhyavyatikrame tadarthatvAnmukhyena vedasaMyogaH" ityadhikaraNe bhASyakAreNa yAjurvedikasyAdhAnasya sAmavede vAravantIyamaGgaM vihitam, tatra vAravantIyasya kiM svavidhinibandhanaM sAmavaidikamuccaistvaM kAryam; uta pradhAnavidhinibaMdhanaM yAjurvedikamupAMzutvamiti sandehe pradhAnavidhinibandhanasya svarasya pradhAnaikavAkyatApekSitvena vilambitatvAt; tadanapekSatayA zIghraM pratIyamAnamuccaistvaM kAryamiti pUrvapakSaM prApayya, rAddhAntitam-vilambitapratItikamapyupAMzutvaM pradhAnadharmatvAdbalIyaH, pradhAne sAkSAnnivezamalabhamAnatayA'GgAvatIrNamapi hi tatpradhAnasyaiva dharmaH | ato'GgadharmamuccaistvaM bAdhitvA pradhAnadharma upAMzutvaM kAryam; anyathA pradhAnavaiguNyApatteriti | evaJca pradhAnakarmoddezena svaravizeSavidhAnAnAM na kevalaM tadaGgamantreSvaniyatasvaraprAptimatsu pradhAnasvaraniyamamAtraM phalaM, kintu niyatasvavidhinibandhanasvaraprAptimatsu vAravantIyAdiSu tadvyAvartanenA'prAptapradhAnasvaraprApaNamapi phalam | atazcApUrvavidhitvaM yuktamiti cet - [p.80] tannirAsaH | astu nAmaivam; tathA'pyaniyatasvaraprAptimanmantrAMze niyamavidhitvamapi vaktavyaM vinigamanAvirahAdityativyAptiranivAryeva | na hyavahanane niyamavidhireva; na parisaMkhyetyatrA'vahanananiyamasya vidalanAdinivRttiM pratyupajIvyatvamiva razanAdAnamantravidhiH parisaMkhyaiva na niyamavidhirityatra gardabharazanAnivRtteranyalabhyatvamiveha vidhireva, na niyama ityatra kiJcidvinigamakamasti; iha tayorupajIvyopajIvakabhAvAbhAvAt, dvayorapyananyalabhyatvAcca | na caikasyaiva viSayabhedena vidhiniyamarUpatve kazciddoSaH, vidhivairUpyAbhAvena pUrvazlokodAhRtasArvakAmyAdivAkyAnAM vidhyanuvAdarUpatvavadeteSAM vidhi-niyamarUpatvasyA'pyupapatteH | ata eva prAgAzrayaviSayabhedena vidhi-niyama-parisaMkhyAnAM saMkaro darzitaH | AtyantikatvaM traikAlikatvaM iti svIkAre pratiprasavavidheranupapattyAzaGkA | syAdetat mAbhUtsarveSUddezyeSvanuvRttirUpA vA sarvAtmanA kvacidapyuddezye'nuvRttirUpA vA sarvoddezyA'nuvRttirAtyantikatvam, kintu traikAlikatvam; trikAlAyAmaprAptau vidhiriti prasiddheriti cet, evaM sati apavAdApavAdavidhirna syAt; tasya prAptApoditapunaHprAptyarthatvAt| tathA hi kRSNalacaroH zrapaNaM zrUyate "ghRte zrapayatI"ti | nedamapUrvazrapaNaM vidhIyate, kintu dArzapUrNamAsikazrapaNaM viklittirUpaprayojanAbhAvAnnivartamAnaM pratiprasUyate; ataH prAkRtAH zrapaNadharmAH karttavyA iti sthApitaM dazame | TupTIkAvalambanena zrapaNavidherapUrvavidhitvAsambhavazaGkA | nanu tatra pratiprasavarUpasya zrapaNavidherapUrvavidhitvaM nA'stItyaGgIkRtaM TupTIkAyAm | tatra hyuktaM-"tasmAtpAkAntaraM naitattvapUrvavidhidoSataH | pratiprasavamAtraM tu lAghavAdvihitaM varam" iti || samAdhAnam | [p.81] satyamuktaM zrapaNAntaraM na vidheyamatyantamabuddhisthasya vidhau tasya buddhyArohaNakalpanAgauravAt | kintu codakAnItatayA svata eva buddhyArUDhamarthAbhAvAnnivartamAnaM prAkRtameva zrapaNaM vidheyamityatra tAtparyam; na tu na pratiprasavavidhirapUrvavidhirityetAvat tAtparyam; tadabhAve zrapaNasyA'poditasya prAptyasambhavena tasyApUrvavidhyantarbhAvaucityAt | anyathA niyama-parisaMkhyA'nantarbhUtasya vidhyantaratvApattyA vidhitraividhyabhaGgaprasaGgAt | pratiprasavavidherudAharaNAntarapradarzanam | evaM "pUSA prapiSTabhAgaH" iti pauSNacarau codakataH prAptasya prAkRtasya peSaNasya prayojanAbhAvAnnivartamAnasya vidhirapi pratiprasavavidhiH | etAdRzAH prAptasyApavAde prasakte tadapavAdArthA vidhayaH; eteSu prAkprAptisattve'pi traikAlikAprAptirnA'stItyavyAptiH | kecitpunaH prasaktaprAptikasya prAgevApavAde prasakte tadapavAdArthAH santi vidhayaH; "apabarhiSaH prayAjAnyajatI"tyAdayaH | tathA hi-avabhRthastAvadgRhamedhIyavadapUrvaH | tatra "apsumantAvAjyabhAgau yajatI"tyAjyabhAgapunaH zravaNenAvabhRthaprayogavidheH klRptopakAraprAkRtAjyabhAganairAkAGkSyatayA codakasyA'kalpanIyatvAt | na ca tadapsuzabdopetasya "apsu me somo'bravIdi"tyAdiyAjyAnuvAkyamantradvayasya vidhAnArtham; [p.82] atha caturdazazlokasyAvatArikA pAkSikatvAbhAva Atyantikatvamiti pakSamAdAya zaMkA | nanu tarhyapAkSikatvamAtyantikatvamastu | yujyate ca niyamavidhivyAvRttyarthasya tasya tathA nirvacanam | "yadi somaM na vindeta pUtIkAnabhiSuNuyAdi"ti zrUyate | ayaM somAlAbhe satyabhAvavidhirUpaH pUtIkAnAM yAgasAdhanatvaviSayako'pUrvavidhirna bhavati, kintu "zrutipramANatvAcchiSTAbhAvenAgame'nyasyAziSTatvAdi" (6|3|4|13) ityadhikaraNoktanyAyena nityasya prArabdhakAmyasya ca somayAgasya somAbhAve'pi yathAzaktyavazyAnuSTheyatayA karmacodanAkSipte yasmin kasmiMzcit dravye'vazyamupAdeye tatrA'pi "sAmAnyaM taccikIrSA hI" [p.87] (3|12|27) tyadhikaraNoktanyAyena somavidhAnasyA'pi kiMcidanugrahasiddhaye katipayasomAvayavAnupravezavati somasadRzadravya evopAdeye bahuSu ca somasadRzadravyeSu satsu niyamavidhirayam-yaH somalatAyA abhAve somajAtiparicchinnAn sakalAvayavAnupAdAtumazaktaH tatsadRzeSu sthitAnvikalAnavayavAnupAdAtumicchati, sa pUtIkagatAnevopAdadIta iti | evaM niyamavidhiriti nirNItaM "niyamArthA punaH zrutiri"ti tRtIyAdhyAyAdhikaraNe "vacanAccAnyAyyamabhAve" (6|3|13|31) iti SaSThAdhyAyAdhikaraNe ca | ayaM ca somalatayA susadRzAnAmanyeSAM sattvAtteSAmeva bahvavayavAnugrahAnurodhena pratinidhitayA prApterISatsomasadRzAnAM pUtIkAnAM tatpratikoTitayA pAkSikaprAptirahitAnAmeva vidhiH, ata evoktaM zAstradIpikAyAm "mukhyAbhAve pratinidheH prAptiH tasyAM cA'vasthAyAM yAvanyAyena susadRzaM niyantumupakramyate tAvat vacanena mandasadRzAH pUtIkA niyamyante; ataH pratinidhiniyamo'yam" iti (zA0 pR0 477) | evaM pUtIkAnAmaprApteH prAptikarambitatvAbhAvAdativyAptiH | vAyavyapazau uttarAdhAramantravidhAvativyAptipratipAdanam | tathA vAyavyapazau zrUyate "hiraNyagarbhaH samavarttatAgra ityAghAramAghArayatI"ti | ayaM prAjApatye pUrvAghAre mantravidhirna bhavati | kintvaindra uttarAghAra iti nirNItaM dazame, "hiraNyagarbhaH pUrvasya mantraliGgAt" "uttarasya vA mantrArthavattvAt" (10|3|2|13,15) iti pUrvottarapakSasUtrAbhyAm | tatrA'yaM siddhAntanyAya uktaH- mantravidhau mantrajanyaM niyamAdRSTamapekSaNIyam, anyathA brAhmaNa-kalpasUtropadiSTavacanAdibhirapyanuSTheyArthasmaraNasambhavena mantraniyamavaiyarthyAt | tattu niyamAdRSTaM prakRtau pUrvAghAre na klRptam, tatra tasyA'mantrakatvAt iti pUrvAghAre mantravidhipakSe'traiva tatkalpanIyam, uttarAghAre tu tatklRptam; tasya prakRtAvapi samantrakatvAt | ataH klRpte niyamAdRSTe mantravizeSamAtravidhAnaM laghvityuttarAghAra eva mantravidhiryuktaH iti | uktaM ca TupTIkAyAm- "mantraniyamAdavazyamadRSTaM kalpanIyaM pUrvAghAre; tattu klRptamuttarAghAre tasminsati kalpayituM na zakyate" iti | evaM cA'tra niyamavidhitvenA'GgIkRte AghAramantrAntaravidhau codakAd "indra Urdhvo'dhvaraH" iti [p.88] prAkRtamantrasyaiva prAptirasti na vidheyamantrasyetyatrA'pyativyAptiH | na ca vikRtau mantrAntaravidhAnasamaye prAptyabhAve'pi prakRtau "indra Urdhvo'dhvaraH" iti mantravidhAnasamaye sarveSAmupAyAntarANAM pakSe prAptirasti; saivA'rthato vikRtAvapi prAptiriti vAcyam | tadAnImapi brAhmaNakalpasUtrAdInAmindraprakAzamantrAntarANAM ca pakSe prAptAvapi prajApatiliGgakasyA'sya mantrasya prAptyabhAvAt | na ca liGgamindre'pyaviziSTamiti bhASye samarthitamiti vAcyam | tatra hi vihitaviniyoganirvAhAyendre'pi kathaM cinmantraM yojayituM tasminnapi sRSTeH prAgekatvenA'vasthAnaM mantrAntare varNitamastItyetAvadudAhRtam, na tu hiraNyagarbhazabdasya tatra svArasikaM liGgaM samarthitam | na vA tatsamarthanaM zakyam, tasya prajApatAveva rUDhatvAt | "prajApatirvai hiraNyagarbhaH prajApateranurUpatvAye"ti vAkyazeSAcca | "hiraNyagarbhaH pUrvasya mantraliGgAdi"ti sUtrakRtApi prajApatAveva liGgasadbhAvasyoktatvAcca | evamanyeSvapyetAdRzeSu niyamavidhiSvativyAptirUhanIyA | iyaM cA'vyAptiH, ativyAptizca prAcIneSvapyAtyantikatvanirvacanapakSeSu samAnA | tasmAdaprAptyAtyantikatvayoranirvacanAdayuktamapUrvavidhilakSaNam || iti SoDazazlokasya vyAkhyA |

[p.88] iti zrImadadvaitavidyAcArya zrImadbharadvAjakulajaladhikaustubhazrImadvizvajidyAjizrIraGgarAjAdhvarivara sUnorappayyadIkSitasya kRtau vidhirasAyanasukhopayojinyAmapUrvavidhilakSaNAkSepaH | niyamavidhilakSaNAkSepaH niyamavidhirnAnAprakAro dRzyate | tasya "niyamaH pAkSike satI"ti vArtikoktena lakSaNena saGgraho na bhavitumarhatIti zaGkA | atha niyamavidhilakSaNamAkSipati- hetuH kutrA'pi kAryaM kvacana tadubhayaM kutracidvidhyupAttam vidhyaspRSTaM niyamyaM kvacidubhayavidhaM pAkSikatvena dRSTam | tasyaiva pratyanIkaM kvacidapi kuhacitpAkSikaM naiva dRSTam nAnArUpaM taditthaM niyamavidhizataM durgrahaM lakSaNena ||17|| [p.89] nAnAprakArastAvanniyamavidhirdRzyate | sa sarvo'pi "niyamaH pAkSike satI"tyanena lakSaNena saMgrahItuM na zakyaH | tathA hi-kvacidekasminkArye'niyatAnekopAyaprAptAvekopAyaniyamaH, yathA "vrIhInavahantI"ti | kvacidekasyopAyasyAniyatAnekakAryaprAptAvekakAryaniyamaH | yathA "yadyonyAM gAyati taduttarayorgAyatI"ti | idaM navamazlokAvatArikAyAmupapAditam | kvacidubhayaniyamaH yathA "yajjuhvAM gRhNAti prayAjebhyastadi"ti | idamapi tatraivopapAditam | yadyonyAmityatra kAryamAtraniyamodAharaNazaMkA | nanu tatroktayA rItyA "yadyonyA"mityatrApyubhayaniyamo labhyate | tathA ca kathaM kAryamAtraniyamaH tadudAharaNam? yathA hi-stotrAGgatvenA'vagatasya sAmnastRcagAnanirvAhAya tatsaMskAryatvenA'niyatAnekargdvayaprAptAvuttarayorniyamaH; tathA stotrAGgatvenAvagatayoruttarayorRcoH saMskArakatvenA'niyatAnekasAmaprAptau yonisAmnA niyama ityapi sambhavati; stotrasya pragItamantrasAdhyastutirUpatvena sAmApekSAvazyaMbhAvAt; viziSya sAmApekSAbhAve'pi sAmasAdhAraNaRgabhivyaJjakApekSAvazyambhAvAcceti cet? ubhayaniyamasyodAharaNasamarthanam | satyam, yatra sAmna iva RcAmapi stotrAGgatvaM prakaraNapAThAdinAvagataM, tatra tathaiva | yatra tu vikRtivizeSe vAmadevyAdipRSThavidhau sAmamAtrasya stotrAGgatvAvagatau tatprakaraNapAThAdirahitApi tatsaMskAryA yoniH prakRtau bRhadyonivat vaizyastome [p.90] kaNvarathantarayonivacca sAmnaH stotrAGgatvanirvAhAya svayamapi tadaGgatvamarthAdApadyate | tatra taduttarayoH prAgavagataM stotrAGgatvaM nAstIti; tannirvAhAyAniyatasAmaprAptyabhAvAnna tatra sAmaniyamaH, kintu sAmna eva stotrIyasya tRcagAnanirvAhAya saMskAryargvizeSaniyama iti kevalaM kAryaniyama eva | ato "yadyonyAM gAyati taduttarayorgAyatI"tyayaM yoniviSamacchandaskayoruttarayorapUrvavidhiH | yonisamacchandaskayostu kramaprakaraNAvagataviniyogayoH kAryopAyadvayaniyamavidhiH, sAmaviniyogAkSiptaviniyogayoH kAryamAtraniyamavidhiriti yuktaM kAryaniyame'pi tadudAharaNam | ubhayaniyamasyodAharaNam | evaM vAjapeye dakSiNAtvena deyAnAM saptadazAnAM rathAnAM madhye "indrasya vajro'si vArtraghnaH" ityAdi yAjurvedikamantraiH sajjIkRtaM rathamadhikRtya pravRtte yajuryuktaM rathamadhvaryave dadAtIti vAkye; "yo vRSTikAmo yo'nnAdyakAmo yaH svargakAmaH sa saubhareNa stuvIte"ti phalArthatvena vihitaM brahmasAmastotrAzrayaM saubharamadhikRtya pravRtteSu "hISiti vRSTikAmAya nidhanaM kuryAt, UrgityannAdyakAmAya, U iti svargakAmAye"ti vAkyeSu cobhayaniyamo draSTavyaH | eSUdAharaNeSu samaprAdhAnyenobhayato niyamalAbhaH | upajIvyopajIvakabhAvenA'pi sa sambhavati | smArtaniyamasyodAharaNam | yathA "yAjanAdhyApanapratigrahairbrAhmaNo dhanamarjayedi"tyatra yAjanAdiSu kRSyAdiSu ca dhanArjanopAyeSvaniyamena prApteSu brAhmaNasya yAjanAdaya eva tadupAyA iti niyamyante, tena brAhmaNAnAmeva yAjanAdayastadupAyA ityapi niyamo labhyate | etadvidhyabhAve hi yajJa-dAnAdhyayanavidhibhiryAjakAdhyApakapratigrahItRsAkAGkSairArtvijyAdisamarthA ye kecidyAjakAdaya AkSipyeran | etadvidhisattve tvasmadIyo'yaM dhanArjanopAya iti yAjanAdiSu svata eva pravRttAnAM brAhmaNAnAM sattvena tata eva nirAkAGkSatayA yatkiJcidaniyatayAjakAdyAkSepo nA'vataratIti | [p.91] evaM tAvanniyamye tatpratisaMbandhini ca vidhivAkyenopAtte kvacidupAyaniyamaH, kvacitkAryaniyamaH, kvacidubhayaniyama iti traividhyaM darzitam | niyamye tatpratisaMbandhinyubhayasminnapi vA tadanupAtte'pi sa dRzyate | tadyathA "puroDAzaM prathayatI"tyatrAnupAtte karttari niyamye niyamaH | "uccairRcA kriyata" ityatrAnupAtte niyamyapratisaMbandhini mantre niyamaH, "vrIhibhiryajete"tyatra niyamye'vahanane tatpratisaMbandhiSu yaveSu cAnupAtteSu niyamaH | evamupAttamanupAttamiti dvividhamapyudAhRtaM niyamyajAtaM pakSaprAptaM dRSTam | kvacinniyamyasya pakSaprAptyabhAve'pi tatpratidvandvinAM pakSaprAptimAtreNa niyamo dRzyate | yathA "yadi somaM na vindeta pUtIkAnabhiSuNuyAdi"ti | atra hi susadRzAnAM bahUnAmaniyataprAptau mandasadRzAnAM pUtIkAnAmaprAptAnAmeva niyamaH; kvacinniyamavidhau kimapi pakSaprAptaM na dRzyate, yathA "hiraNyagarbhaH samavarttatAgre" ityAghAramAghArayatIti | tatra niyamatazcodakA"dindra Udhrvo'dhvara" iti mantraprAptAvindraliGgarahitatvAdatyantAprAptameva mantrAntaramaindrAghAre niyamyate | etAnyudAharaNAni prAk tatra tatrA''viSkRtAnIti neha prApaJcitAni | evaM nyAyataulyAt "iSe tve"ti zAkhAmAcchinatti "utkare vAjinamAsAdayati" "paridhau pazuM niyuJjIte"ti vAkyeSvapi niyamavidhiraGgIkaraNIya iti; tAnyapi pakSaprAptirahitodAharaNAni | hiraNyagarbhamantravidherniyamatvasamarthanam | yathA khalu hiraNyagarbhamantrasya prajApatiliGgasyaindrAghAre svayamaprAptasyaiva viniyogAnantaraM tannirvAhAya kathaM cidindraprakAzakatvasya kalpanIyatve'pi dRSTArthatvAnniyamAdRSTakalpanAvazyaMbhAvAcca niyamavidhitvamabhyupagamyate | tathA "iSe tve"ti mantrasya zAkhAcchedane, tIkSNAgrasyotkarasya vAjinAsAdane tanIyasaH paridheH pazuniyojane ca viniyogAnantarameva tannirvAhAya mantre "chinadmI"tyadhyAhArasya utkare pRthvagratvasya, paridhau prathimnazcApekSaNIyatve'pi nyAyasAmyAnniyamavidhitvamabhyupagantavyameva | itthaM nAnArUpA niyamavidhayaH kathamanena saMgrahItuM lakSaNena zakyAH | [p.92] pAkSike satItyasyA'rthAntaravarNanena niyamavidhAvavyAptyudbhAvanam | pAkSikatve satItyasya kAryopAyadvayapAkSikatvaparatve ekaikaniyamAvyApteH; kAryasyaivopAyasyaiva vA pAkSikatvaparatve tadanyataraniyamAvyApteH | kAryopAyadvayamanugamayya vidhyupAttasya pAkSikatvaparatve vidhyanupAttaniyamAvyApteH; upAttamanupAttaM vA anugamayya niyamyasya pAkSikatvoktau niyamyapratidvandvinAmeva pAkSikatvavanniyamavidhAvavyApteH | niyamyasyAnyasya vA yasya kasya citpAkSikatvaparatvoktAvapi pAkSikatvamAtrarahiteSu niyamavidhiSvavyApterarthaH | zloke "tasyaiva pratyanIkaM kvacidapI"tyatra pAkSikatvena dRSTamityanuSaGgaH ||17|| iti saptadazazlokavyAkhyA | athASTAdazazlokasyAvatArikA | niyamavidhilakSaNasyAtivyAptyudbhAvanam | evaM lakSaNasyAvyAptimuktvA'tivyAptimAha- taccA'tivyAptamagnervikRtijuSi vidhau mIlanasyA'gratAyAH naimittikyAzca vidhyorakhilavidhiSu ca prApakAvartakeSu | aprAptAMzasya pUrtiH phalamapi yadi tatrA'dhikaM yojanIyaM zeSAMzavyarthatA syAdakhilaphalakatAvidhyativyApanaM ca ||18|| yadi pAkSike satItyatra vidhiviSayasya pAkSikatvaM vivakSitaM tadA vaikRtAgnividhAvativyAptiH; tadviSayasyAgneruttaravedyA vikalpitasya pAkSikatvAt | yadi vidhyuddezyasya; tadA rathantare prastUyamAne saMmIlayediti vidhAvativyAptiH, taduddezyarathantarasya jyotiSTome pAkSikatvAt | yadyubhayoH; tadA "yadi rathantarasAmA somaH syAdaindravAyavAgrAn grahAn gRhNIyAdi"ti prAgudAhRtaparisaMkhyAyAmativyAptiH; tatroddezyarathantarasAmatvasya tannimittakasya vidheyasyaindravAyavAgratvasya ca prakRtau [p.93] pAkSikatvAt | yadi vidheyoddezyasAdhAraNyena vidhyupAttasya pAkSikatvaM vivakSitam tadodAhRteSu triSvapi vidhiSvativyAptiH | yadi vidhyupAttatadanupAttasAdhAraNyena niyamyapAkSikatvaM vivakSitaM tadA niyamavidhilakSaNe niyamyapraveze AtmAzrayApattiH| yadi niyamyazabdena prakRtavidhiphalAzrayamAtraM vivakSaNIyaM, tathA satyudAhRte naimittikAgratAvidhAvativyAptiH | tatra vikRtivizeSavyAvRttiphalAzrayasyaindravAyavAgratvasya pAkSikatvAt| yadi niyamyAniyamyasAdhAraNyena yasya kasya citpAkSikatvaM vivakSitam; tadA sarveSvapyapUrvavidhi-parisaMkhyAvidhiSvativyAptiH | pAkSike satItyatrAprAptAMzapUraNaphalakatvavizeSaNadAnena pUrvoktAtivyAptivAraNe'pi zrutAMzasya vaiyarthyApAdanam | syAdetat- yasya kasya citpAkSikatve sati tadaprAptAMzapUraNaphalo niyamavidhirityadhikaM yojyate; tathA sati na kvApyativyAptiriti cet, evaM tarhi lakSaNe zrutAMzo vyarthaH syAt; aprAptAMzapUraNaphalo niyamavidhirityetAvata eva viziSTalakSaNapUrvavattvAt | na caivamevA'stviti vAcyam; "sarvebhyo jyotiSToma" iti sarvaphalakatvavidhAvativyApteH | prAtisvikavAkyena svargArthatve prApte tasya tadaprAptaphalAntarAprAptAMzapUraNArthatvAdityarthaH | ityaSTAdazazlokasya vyAkhyA athaikonaviMzazlokasyAvataraNikA | aprAptervirodhyAkramaNaprayuktatvavivakSayAtivyAptinirasanakathanam | syAdetat-aprAptAMzapUraNaphalako niyamavidhiriti lakSaNe aprAptatvaM virodhyAkramaNaprayuktatvaM vivakSitam | ato noktAtivyAptiH | sArvakAmyavidhau svargAnyaphalAMzasyA'prApteH prApakapramANAbhAvaprayuktatvena virodhyAkramaNaprayuktatvAbhAvAt | na cA'sambhavaH | niyamavidhyudAharaNe'vahananAdiprApakalaukikapramANasattve'pi tadaprAptervidalanAdivirodhyAkramaNaprayuktatvAt | [p.94] aprAptAMzasyAnirdhAritatvavizeSaNadAnenAtivyAptiparihArakathanam | yadvA'nirdhAritatvamaprAptAMzasya vizeSaNaM kriyate | tathA ca sArvakAmyavidhau svargAMzaH prAptaH tadanyaphalAMzastvaprApta iti nirdhAraNasadbhAvAnnAtivyAptiH | nApyasambhavaH | vrIhyAdiSvavahananAdyaprAptAMzasyetadantayA nirdhAraNAbhAvAdityAzaGkyAha- aprAptatvaM virodhyAkramaNakRtamihA'pekSyate yadyathA'pi syAddoSo'yaM hi pRSThyaH SaDaha iti vidhAvapyayaM nirnirodhaH | aprAptAMzaM vizeSTuM kriyata iti mataM yadyanirdhAritatvam zyAvAzvabrAhmaNoktiprabhRtividhigaNAtiprasaktistathA'pi ||19|| prathamavivakSAyAM kRtAyAmapyudbhAvitasArvakAmyavidhyativyAptidoSastadavastha eva; vizvajidadhikaraNapUrvapakSanyAyasya svargAnyaphalAnAmapi prApakapramANasya sattvena tadaprAptestannyAyApavAdakasvargakAmavidhiprApti-tadvirodhyAkramaNaprayuktatvAt | aprAptatvaM virodhyAkramaNaprayuktatvamiti prathamapakSe'tivyAptyudbhAvanam | nanu-aprAptikarambitavirodhyAkramaNaprayuktatvaM vivakSitam | asti ca vrIhyAdiSvavahananAdyaprAptAvAkSepakRtAprAptikarambitaprAptikavidalanAdirUpavirodhyAkramaNaprayuktatvam | evaM ca na sArvakAmyavidhAvativyAptistadabhAve svargAnyaphalAprApteH svargakAmavAkyakRtaniyataprAptikasvargarUpavirodhyAkramaNaprayuktatvAditi cet, evamapi "pRSThyaH SaDaho bRhadrathantarasAmA kArya" iti pRSThyaSaDahoddezena bRhadrathantarapRSThavidhAvativyAptiranivAryA | SaDahAMzeSu tRtIyAdiSvahassu, bRhadrathantarAprApteraprAptikarambitaprAptikavairUpavairAjAdirUpavirodhyAkramaNaprayuktatvAt | anirdhAritatvamaprAptAMzasya vizeSaNamiti dvitIyaprakArasya dUSaNam | [p.95] dvitIyavivakSAyAM yadyapi noktAtivyAptiH; bRhadrathantarAdyaprAptAMzastRtIyasutyAdirUpa iti nirdhAraNasambhavAt | tathApi "azvaH zyAvo dakSiNe"tyupahavyadakSiNAM vidhAya zrAvite "sa brahmaNe deya" iti vidhau; "audumbarassomacamaso dakSiNe"ti dakSiNAM vidhAya Rtapeye zrAvite "sa priyAya sagotrAya brahmaNe deya" iti vidhau; AyuH-kAmeSTiprakaraNazrute "sarvaM brahmaNe pariharatI"ti vidhau cAtivyAptiH, sarvaRtviksAdhAraNeSvazva-somacamasa-haviHzeSeSvayaM brahmaNo'MzaH, ayamanyeSAmiti nirdhAraNAsambhavAt | tasmAdavyAptyativyAptigrastatvAnniyamavidhilakSaNamapi na yuktamiti bhAvaH ||19|| ityekonaviMzazlokavyAkhyA

[p.95] iti zrImadadvaitavidyAcArya zrImadbharadvAjakulajaladhikaustubhazrImadvizvajidyAjizrIraGgarAjAdhvarivarasUnorappayyadIkSitasya kRtau vidhirasAyanasukhopayojinyAM niyamavidhilakSaNAkSepaH | parisaMkhyAlakSaNamAkSipati- tatrA'nyatreti rAzidvitayamabhihitaM lakSaNe pArisaMkhye pazyAmastatra lakSyopagatamanugataM bhinnadharmopagUDham | pAThaprAptAgratAvidhyanugatirahitaM vidhyupAttAnupAttaM grAhyaM vyAvartanIyaM tadapi ca na bhajatyAvapantIti lakSyam ||20|| zeSi-zeSabhedena parisaMkhyAyA dvaividhyanirUpaNam | "tatra ca anyatra ca prApta" iti parisaMkhyAlakSaNamitthaM khalu lakSyeSu yojyate| "imAmagRbhNan razanAmRtasyetyazvAbhidhAnImAdatte" iti lakSye tatra-azvarazanAgrahaNe [p.96] anyatra-gardabharazanAgrahaNe ca zeSiNi, ekasminmantrarUpe zeSe mantraliGgAvizeSAtprApte sati gardabharazanAgrahaNarUpazeSyantaraparisaMkhyA | "daNDI praiSAnanvAhe"ti lakSye tatra-praiSAnuvacane, anyatra-rAtripracArAdike ca sthitvoccairanavAnakaraNAya, tamovagAhanAdisaukaryAya ca kiJcidavalambanApekSe maitrAvaruNakArye zeSiNi maitrAvaruNagRhItadIkSitadaNDarUpe ekasmin zeSe prApte, rAtripracArAdizeSyantaraparisaMkhyA | zeSaparisaMkhyAyA udAharaNam "zaMyvantA prAyaNIyA santiSThate" iti lakSye prAyaNIyAyAmekasyAM zeSiNyAM zaMyvantAGgakalAparUpe; taduparitanAGgakalAparUpe ca zeSe prApte taduparitanAGgakalAparUpazeSAntaraparisaMkhyA | evam "iDAntA''tithyA santiSThata" ityAdAvapIti | evaJcA'zvarazanAgrahaNa-praiSAnuvacana-zaMyviDAntAGgakalApAdikamekarAziM, gardabharazanAgrahaNarAtripracArazaMyviDoparitanAGgakalApAdikamaparaM ca rAziM kRtvA; tadrAzidvayaM [p.103] atha trayoviMzazlokasyAvatArikA | syAdetat- "tatra cAnyatra ca prApta" etadanyataranivRttiphalako vidhiH parisaMkhyeti phalamapyadhikaM lakSaNe yojayiSyAmaH | tathA codAhRteSvaprAptaviSayeSu catuSSaDahAdividhiSu tattatprAptereva phalatvena pakSaprAptaviSayeSvavahananAdividhiSu tattanniyamanasyaiva phalatvena nivRtterAnuSaGgikatvAnnAtivyAptirityAzaGkyAha- parisaMkhyAlakSaNe vyAvRttiphalakatvanivezena pUrvoktadUSaNanirAkaraNam | yojyastatraiva bAdhaH phalamapi yadi tadvA'rtha evA'vaziSTaH tasyA'pyuktyA niSedhAtmakavidhiniyamAtiprasakterarodhAt | prAptaM sarvatra kiJcitkhalu sulabhataraM varjanIyena sAkaM gRhyante te'pi lakSyA yadi tadapi vidhau sarvathAtiprasaktiH ||23|| yadi lakSaNe nivRttiphalakatvamapyadhikaM nivezyate; tarhyavaziSTaH zrutAMzo vyarthaH, vidhi-niyamayoH prApti-pUrtiphalakayornivRttiphalakatvAbhAvena tAvataiva sarvAti[p.104]vyAptivAraNAt | syAdetat- niSedhAtmakA api santi vidhayo niyamAzca, yathA "dIkSito na juhoti" "na tau pazau karoti" "nA'tirAtre SoDazinaM gRhNAtI"tyAdayo vidhiprAptAnAM niSedhAnAM vidhayaH, dIkSAmadhye'gnihotrahomAnAm, pazAvAjyabhAgayoH atirAtre SoDazigrahasya ca varjanAnAmatyantAprAptatvAt | "na hiMsyAt" "nAnRtaM vadet" ityAdayo rAgaprAptAnAM niSedhA niyamAH | hiMsAdineva kadA cittadvarjanenA'pi puruSasyepsitasaMbhavena tasyA'pi pakSe prAptatvAt tatrA'nyanivRttimAtraphalakasyA'tivyApteH, tatra cA'nyatra samuccitya prApta ityapi vizeSaNaM kriyata iti cet| maivam | teSvativyApteH | tasya vizeSaNatvasyoktyA'pi nirodhAsambhavAt | sarvatra hi kiJcitkiJcidavarjanIyaM varjanIyena saha samuccitya prAptaM sulabhameva yathA dIkSAmadhye kratvarthahomAH puruSArthahomairagnihotrAdibhiH saha prAptAH pazAvAjyabhAgavyatiriktAGgAnyAjyabhAgAbhyAM saha prAptAni | atirAtre SoDazigrahavyatiriktAGgAni SoDazigraheNa saha prAptAni puruSe bhojanAdIni hiMsAdibhiH saha prAptAni | atyantAprAptanivRttiphalakatvamiti lakSaNasya vivakSA | nanu "tatra cAnyatra ca prApta" ityatraikarUpasaMbandhena prAptirvivakSitA | asti ca prAgudAhRteSu sarveSu parisaMkhyodAharaNeSu zeSatvena zeSitvenA''zrayatvena nimittatvena vA yena kena cidekarUpeNaiva saMbandhenobhayoH prAptiH | tathA ca "dIkSito na juhotI"tyatrA'pUrvavidhAvuktavivakSayA'tivyAptinirAsaH, dIkSAmadhye kratvarthahomAnAM kratuzeSatvena prAptiH; puruSArthahomAnAM kAlaikyeneti saMbandhabhedAt | "na tau pazau karoti" "nAtirAtre" iti niSedhayoruktalakSaNAnusAreNa parisaMkhyAtvamevA'stu | na caivamapi na hiMsyAditi niyamavidhAvativyAptiH, puruSaM prati zeSatvenaiva bhojanAdInAM hiMsAdInAM ca prAptisattvAditi vAcyam | atyantA'prAptanivRttiphalakatvasya lakSaNe vivakSitatvAditi cennaivam | apUrvavidhAvativyAptyudbhAvanam [p.105] dRSTArthAnAM kaNDUyanAdInAm, adRSTArthAnAM ca hariharasmaraNAdInAM puruSArthahomaiH saha kratau kAlaikyasaMbandhena prAptisattvena "dIkSito na juhoti" ityapUrvavidhAvuktavivakSayApyativyAptyanirAsAt | nivRttAvatyantAprAptatvavizeSaNavivakSAyAM zazAdipaJcakavyatiriktapaJcanakhalakSaNanivRtteratyantAprAptatvAbhAvena "paJca paJcanakhA bhakSyA" iti parisaMkhyAyAmavyAptiprasaktyA tasya vizeSaNasyA'vivakSaNIyatayA "na hiMsyAditi" niyamavidhyativyAptyanirAsAcca | dIkSito na juhotItyAdInAM parisaMkhyAtvazaMkA | nanu yadi "dIkSito na juhotI"tyAdiSu sarveSu niSedheSu lakSaNamasti, tadA santu te'pi parisaMkhyA iti cet, tathApyapUrvavidhAvativyAptiH | yadi nivRttiphalako vidhirityetAvadeva lakSaNaM kriyate; yadi vAnekasya samuccitya prAptAviti vizeSyate, sarvathApyagnihotrAdividhAvastyevedaM lakSaNam | zramaparihArAdyarthaM laukikakarmaNAmagnihotrAdikarmaNAJcA'karaNayoH samuccitya prAptau "yAvajjIvamagnihotraM juhoti" ityAdividheragnihotrAdyakaraNanivRttirUpatadanuSThAnaphalakatvAt | na cA'tra nivRttipadenAbhAvarUpaiva nivRttirvivakSiteti zakyaM vaktum | prAgudAhRtaniSedhaparisaMkhyAsvanuSThAnaphalAsvavyAptiprasaGgAt | tasmAtparisaMkhyAlakSaNamapi na yuktamiti bhAvaH | iti trayoviMzazlokavyAkhyA iti zrImadadvaitavidyAcArya zrImadbharadvAjakulajaladhikaustubhazrImadvizvajidyAjizrIraGgarAjAdhvarivarasUnorappayyadIkSitasya kRtau vidhirasAyanasukhopayojinyAM parisaMkhyAvidhilakSaNAkSepaH | [p.106] vilakSaNAnyapyuditAni lakSaNAnyapUrvavidhyAdivibhAgasiddhaye | bhajanti bhUmneha kRtAM padAvaliM parasparAmapyaparasparAmimAm || apUrvavidhilakSaNAkSepANAM niyamavidhilakSaNe'tidezaH, niyamavidhilakSaNAkSepANAM parisaMkhyAlakSaNe'tidezaH, evaM parisaMkhyAvidhilakSaNAkSepANAM niyamavidhilakSaNe cAtidezaH iti saMkSepeNa kathanam | tasyaiva vistareNa pratipAdanaM caturviMzena zlokena | evaM saMkSiptamAkSepAtidezametatprapaJcanahetUpanyAsavyAjena sAmAnyataH pradarzayati- pUrvAkSepaprakAraH prabhavati paramapyAtmavazyaM vidhAtum prAyaH sUkSmaM vibhAgaM vidhiSu gamayatAM lakSaNAnAmamISAm | zakyaM tannyAyasAmyaM praNihitamatibhirboddhamityapradarzya triSvapyeteSu bhinnasthitiSu navanavo varNitastatprakAraH ||24|| apUrvavidhyAdivibhAjakAnAmudAhRtalakSaNAnAM madhye pUrvasya apUrvavidhilakSaNasya ya AkSepaprakAraH; sa prAyeNa paraM niyamavidhilakSaNamapyAskandituM prabhavati| tadyathA-pAkSike satItyatra vidhiviSayasya pAkSikatvaM vivakSitaM, yasya kasya cidvA ? Adye sarvatra pAkSikatvavivakSAyAM vikRtivizeSagatarathantarapRSThaniyamavidhAvavyAptiH, vikRtyantare'pi tathaiva niyamavidhisattvena tadvidhyabhAve rathantarapRSThasya sArvatrikapAkSikatvAbhAvAt | (tantravA0 711 pRSTha) iti vArttikoktaM viziSTavidhipakSamavalambyA'zvAbhidhAnyuddezena vidhAnAnnA'zvAbhidhAnIpade'pUrvIyatvalakSaNAprasaktiriti parihartuM zakyam, tathApyayaM parihAro na sArvatrikaH; niyacchanniyamavidhiH; avahananavidhiravahananasya tadeva prayacchannapUrvavidhirastu, prokSaNasya hi mantraliGgakalpyazrutividheyatve kalpyazrutAvAdhvaryavAdisamAkhyAnAbhAvAttatrA'niyamena yaH kazcit [p.119] atha saptaviMzatizlokasyAvatArikA | parisaMkhyodAharaNAkSepaH | uktanyAyena parisaMkhyodAharaNamapi kartRniyamArthameva syAt; na tu parisaMkhyArthamiti; tadudAharaNamapyayuktamityAha- evaJcA'zvAbhidhAnIgrahavidhirapi tatkartRnaiyatyamAtraprAptyarthaH paryavasyetprathanavidhiriva prAptamantrAnuvAdI | mantre'pyanyAprasaktyai vidhimiha saguNaprAkRtAGgoktanItiH na kSAmyenmantramAtre sati vidhiviSaye syAdviziSTAnuvAdaH ||27|| vidhyantarAvakAzasattve jaghanyaparisaMkhyArthatvaM na svIkarttavyamiti prAguktam | prokSaNAvahananavidhyozca kartRniyamakartRprAptyarthatayA cAritArthyamuktam; prathanasya puroDAzAnyathAnupapattilabhyatvAt | nA'pi tatroruprathasveti mantravidhiH; tasya mantraliGgAdeva prathane prAptisattvAt, kiM tvanyalabhyaprathanavidhAnadvArA''dhvaryukartRkatvaniyamArthastatra mantragrahaNaM liGgaprAptAnuvAda iti hyuktaM mantrAdhikaraNe | [p.120] "azvAbhidhAnImAdatta" ityatra na parisaMkhyetyAzaMkA| nanu prathanavidhau liGgaprAptasya mantrasya vidhAne prayojanAbhAvAdastvanuvAdaH | iha tu liGgAtprApnuvanmantro gardabharazanAyAmapi prasajyeteti tadvyAvartanena mantravidheH prayojanavattvAdiha mantrAMze'pi vidhiH syAt | tallabhyaparisaMkhyAyAH samAkhyAlabhyakartRniyamAvirodhitvena tadarthatve parisaMkhyArthatvaM nA'bhyupagantavyamiti nirbandhAbhAvAditi cet - satyamidam | parisaMkhyAlAbhArthaM mantrasyA'pi vidhAnaM vikRtiSu saguNaprAkRtAGgapunaHzravaNAnyadhikRtya "vikRtau zabdavattvAtpradhAnasya guNAnAmadhikotpattiH sannidhAnAdi"ti (10|3|1|1) iti dAzamikAdhikaraNe pradarzitanyAyo na sahate | "ekAdazaprayAjAnyajati" "hiraNyagarbhaH samavarttatAgre" ityAghAramAghArayatItyAdau yatra prAkRtaM prayAjAghArAdikamaGgaM vaikRtasaMkhyA mantrAdiguNayuktaM zrUyate;
