[p.123] athaikonatriMzazlokasyAvataraNikA | nanu "imAmagRbhNanni"ti mantrasyAdhvaryavasamAkhyAnenaiva tatprakAzyasya grahaNasyAdhvaryukartRkatvaniyamaH sidhyati | atastadarthamarthaprAptagrahaNavidheranapekSitatvAdvAkyasyArthavattvAya parisaMkhyAvidhirevA'GgIkaraNIya iti cet- maivam | adhvaryUccAritamantreNA'nyasyA'pyanuSTheyArthasmaraNasambhavena tAvatA grahaNasyA'dhvaryukartRkatvAsiddheH | ata eva prathanamantrasyA''dhvaryavatve'pi prathanasyAdhvaryavatvasiddhyarthaM prathane vidhiraGgIkRto mantrAdhikaraNe | ata eva mantrakalpasUtrAditaulyenopadiSTavacanasyA'nuSTheyArthasmaraNopAyatvena pakSaprAptirapi darzitA | razanAgrahaNasyAdhvaryukartRkatvasidhyabhAvavatprokSaNAvahananavidhyoH yavaparisaMkhyArthatvamevetyupapAdanam | nanu mAbhUnmantrayoH samAkhyayA prathana-razanAgrahaNayoradhvaryukartRkatvasiddhiH; prokSaNAvahananayostu syAdeva | prokSAmi vadhyAsamityuttamapuruSeNa pratIyamAnasya mantroccArayitRkartRkatvasya bAdhakAbhAvena tyAgAyogAditi cet, evaM tarhi prokSaNAvahananavidhyoH prathamamupapAditaM yavaparisaMkhyArthatvamevA'stu; mAbhUnniyamApUrvavidhitvam | na ca tathA sati vrIhizAstraM vyarthaM syAt; prokSaNAvahananavidhyorvrIhipadasyA'pUrvIyatvalakSaNayA yavasAdhAraNyApAdanenaiva tasya phalavattvAditi vAcyam | tasyA'zvazaphaparimANapuroDAzotpAdanAsamarthAlpavrIhilAbhe pratinidhyanupAdAnArthatayA cAritArthyasaMbhavena yavasAdhAraNyApAdanaphalakatvasyA'nabhyupagantavyatvAt | api ca vrIhizAstreNA'pi prokSaNAvahananayoryavasAdhAraNyaM kartuM na zakyamiti tasya tatphalatvAbhyupagama eva na yukta ityabhipretyAha- [p.124] ye vrIhiprokSaNAderyavamapi viSayaM tanvate tacca cintyam yuktA'pUrveNa dadhyAnayanavidhinayAdarthavattA vidhInAm | no cennaimittiko'pi pratinidhivadiyAtsomadharmopadezAn RcchetsammIlanAdInapi ca bRhadapi stutyapUrvAvibhedAt ||29|| vrIhInprokSatItyAdau vrIhitvAvacchinnoddezena prokSaNavidhAnaM na tvapUrvasAdhanIbhUtavrIhyuddezeneti pratipAdanam | "vrIhIn prokSati" "vrIhInavahantI"ti apUrva-niyamavidhyudAharaNayorvrIhyuddezena vidhIyamAnaM prokSaNamavahananaJca yavasAdhAraNaM; vrIhipadasyApUrvIyaparatvAditi siddhAntamaryAdA'pyanupapannA | vrIhipadasya mukhyArthaparatvaM parityajyA'pUrvIyAkAralakSakatvaM kimarthaM kalpanIyam ? na tAvattadarthasya dvitIyAzrutyavagataprokSaNAvahananasAdhyatvanirvAhArtham; "vrIhInprokSati" "vrIhInavahantI"ti laukikaprokSaNAvahananaviSayapauruSeyavAkya iva tasya jalasaMyoga-tuSavimokarUpalaukikaphalaviziSTatayA tatsAdhyatvopapatteH | nA'pi tasya prokSaNajanyAdRSTasaMskArAvahanananiyamasAdhyatvanirvAhArtham; tatsAdhyatvasyAzrutatvAt | kriyAsAdhyatvarUpamAtraM hi dvitIyAzrutyavagataM karmatvam | na tu kriyAjanyAtizayatanniyamasAdhyatvaparyantam | nanu tathA'pi prokSaNajanyasaMskArAvahanananiyamayorvaiphalyaparihArAya tatsAdhyatvaM vrIhizabdasamarpitasyaiva vaktavyamiti tadarthamapUrvIyAkAralakSaNeti cenna, yadarthA vrIhayaH tasminnapUrva eva tayorapyupayogasambhavAt | "prAGmukho'nnAni bhuJjIte"tyatra hi prAGmukhatvaniyamo bhojanasvarUpAnupayogI; bhojanasyA'pUrvArthatvAbhAvena tadgatApUrvIyatvarUpopayogItyapi samarthayitumazakyaH sannaklRptApUrvopayogI kalpyate | iha klRpte dArzapUrNamAsike prakaraNinyapUrve viparivarttamAne tayostadupayogitvaM kathaM kalpyeta | nanu ca prokSaNAvahananayorapUrvIyavrIhyarthatvAbhAve tadvAkyayorvrIhipadaM laukikApUrvIyasAdhAraNyena vrIhimAtraparaM vA syAt, laukikavrIhiparaM vA ? Adye vidhivairUpyApattiH - apUrvIyavrIhiSu prokSaNAvahananayorviniyuktasaMskAratayA guNa[p.125]karmatvasya laukikavrIhiSu bhUtabhAviviniyogarahiteSvarthakarmatvasya ca prApteH | dvitIye dvitIyAzrutyavagatadravyaprAdhAnyabhaGgApattiH | tasmAdvidhivairUpyaparihArAya zrutaguNapradhAnabhAvanirvAhAya prokSaNAvahananayoH sAmavAyikAGgatvalAbhAya ca vrIhipadasyA'pUrvIyavrIhiparatvAvazyaMbhAve sati tatra laukikavrIhivyAvarttakasyA'pUrvIyatvasyAvazyaM vivakSaNIyatvAdvrIhitvasyA'pi vivakSAyAM viziSTAnuvAdadoSApatteH; apUrvIyatvasya ca yavasAdhAraNatvAtprokSaNAvahananayoryavasAdhAraNyamavarjanIyamiti cet - vrIhipadasya vrIhimAtraparatve'pyapUrvasAdhanatvasyArthasiddhatvena viziSTAnuvAdadoSanirAsaH | maivam | vrIhipadasya vrIhimAtraparatve'pi tatra laukikavrIhivyAvarttakasyApUrvIyatvasyA'rthAllAbhasambhavena viziSTAnuvAdadoSAprasaGgAt | tathA hi- adhikAravAkyasya prakArAkAGkSAyAmavahananavAkyasya phalAkAGkAyAJcAvahananavAkyasyApi samidAdivAkyavadadhikAravAkyenaikavAkyatA kalpanIyA | tatazca tasyaivamarthaH saMpadyate avahanena vrIhidvAreNa svargArthadarzapUrNamAsayAgApUrvaM prati vyApriyeteti | na cA'tatsAdhanabhUtavrIhidvArA tatprati vyApartuM zakyate | tenA'rthAdya eva yAgArthaM niruptA vrIhayasteSAmevAvahananamiti vyavasthA sidhyati | etena prokSaNamapi vyAkhyAtam | evamarthAllabhye prokSaNAvahananayorapUrvasAdhanavrIhiviSayatve vrIhipadasya zrutavrIhitvaparityAgenA'pUrvasAdhanatvena lakSaNA kalpayitumanucitA | avazyaJca laukikasAdhAraNenAkAreNa zabdasamarpita evApUrvIyapadArthe saMskArakarmAnvayabodhane sati tadanantaraM tasyA'pUrvIyatvAvagatAvarthAtsaMskArasyA'pUrvIyaviSayatvaparyavasAnamityayaM nyAyaH- "tapte payasi dadhyAnayatI"tyatrAGgIkaraNIyaH | tatra hi payaH saMskAratayA dadhyAnayanaM vidhIyate; taddvAraM tu bhinnamanityameveti nityAnityasaMyogavirodhaH | tasmAtpratinidhInAM mukhyasAmAnavidhyam; naimittikAnAM nityasAmAnavidhyAbhAvaJca vyavasthayA'bhyupagacchadbhirmImAMsakaiH saMskAravAkyagatavrIhisomAdipadAnAmapUrvIyatvalakSaNAM parityajya mukhyArthaparatvamevA'bhyupetya pratinidhigatamukhyAvayavAnAM vrIhyAdigatatadavayavAnAmiva vrIhyAdipadamukhyatvAnmukhyasAmAnavidhyam, phalacamasasya somapadaviSayatvAbhAvAnna somasAmAnavidhyamityeva sA vyavasthopapAdanIyA | pratIyate ca tattadadhikaraNaparyAlocanayA tathaivA'bhyupetya sA vyavasthopapAditeti | kathamiti cet? ucyate | "naimittikamatulyatvAdasamAnavidhAnaM syAdi"ti (3|6|13) tArtIyAdhikaraNe "yadi rAjanyaM vaizyaM vA yAjayetsa yadi somaM bibhakSayiSennyagrodhastibhIrAhRtya tAH saMpiSya dadhanyunmRjya tamasmai bhakSaM prayacchenna somami"ti vAkyamudAhRtyA'tra rAjanya-vaizyanimittena somasthAne vidhIyamAnaH phalacamasaH saMskAravAkyeSu somena samAnavidhAnaH syAdutAsamAnavidhAna iti saMzayya; tadgatA vrIhyavayavA eva tu sakalAsambhavAdvikalA upAdIyante; nIvArAvayavopAdAnaM tvanuSaGgAdavarjanIyatayA| vrIhyavayavAzca vrIhigatA iva nIvAragatA api vrIhizabdaviSayatayA saMskAravidhyanvitA eva | ataH samAnavidhAnA iti "tadvatprayojanaikatvAdi"tisUtreNa siddhAntitam | anenA'pi saMskAravAkyeSu vrIhyAdizabdAnAM mukhyArthavivakSA spaSTamevA'vasIyate| apUrvIyatvavivakSAyAM nIvArAdikasyApyapUrvIyatvena saMskAravidhyanvayasya sUpapAdatayA teSAM sAdhanatvanirAkaraNasyAnupayogaprasaGgAt | syAdetat- naimittika-pratinidhyornityAnityasaMyogavirodhaprasaGgatadaprasaGgau na mUlayuktitvenAsAmAnavidhyasAmAnavidhyavyavasthApakau; [p.131] vrIhizAstra-yavazAstrayoH yugapatpravRttyA'vahananAdInAM yavasAdhAraNyena naimittikazAstrANAM nityazAstrasya ca paurvAparyeNa pravRttyA tatsAdhAraNyAbhAva iti kathanam | nanu tarhi vrIhizAstrAdipravRttyanantaramevA'vahananAdInAM tadanvayo'stu | tathA ca vrIhizAstrAdyavagatApUrvIyatvAkAreNa teSAM vrIhyAdyanvaya upapadyate | yavazAstrasyApyanaimittikatvena vrIhizAstrasamakAlapravRttikatayA yavAnAmapyapUrvIyatvena kroDIkArAdyavasAdhAraNyamapi teSAmupapadyate | naimittikazAstrANAM tu nityazAstrapravRttyanantarabhAvitvena saMskArANAM nityAnvayavelAyAM naimittikagatApUrvIyatvAnavagatyA tatsAdhAraNyAbhAvazcopapadyata iti sarvaM samaJjasamiti cet - dRSTAntadArSTAntikavaiSamyapradarzanam, tena naimittikAnAM nityasAmAnavidhyApAdanaM ca | evaM hyanaimittikatvena yavazAstravadeva vrIhizAstrasamakAlaM pravRttiyogyAnAmavahananAdizAstrANAM yavasAdhAraNyArthameva yavazAstrasyApi pravRttyanantaraM pravRttirabhyupagamyata ityAyAtam | tathA nityadharmANAM naimittikasAdhAraNyArthaM naimittikazAstrasyA'pi pravRttyanantaraM saMskAravAkyAnAM pravRttirityapi kuto nA'bhyupagamyate | vastutastu "vrIhibhiryajete"tyAdizAstrairmukhyAnAM vrIhyAdInAmiva nIvArAdigatavrIhyAdyavayavAnAM nA'pUrvIyatvaM sidhyati | vrIhitvAdijAtivAcinAM vrIhyAdizabdAnAM tadavacchinnavrIhyAdilakSakatvasyeva tadanavacchinnanIvArAdigatavrIhyAdibhAvayogyAvayavalakSakatvasyA'sambhavAt | kintu vrIhizAstrAdyanusAreNa mukhyeSveva vrIhyAdiSvavazyamupAdAtavyeSu nityam, ArabdhakAmyaM ca kadAcidvrIhyAdyalAbhe'pyavazyamanuSTheyamiti pradhAnAnusAreNa yatkiJcidravyAkSepe ca prasakte vrIhitvAdijAtyanavacchinnatve'pi tadavacchedyatvayogyatayA mukhyasannikRSTAnAM nIvArAdigatavrIhyAdibhAvayogyAvayavAnAM sambhave viprakRSTamASAdidravyAntaropAdAnAyogAtta evopAdeyA ityevaMrUpAdAkSepAtteSAmapUrvIyatvaM sidhyati | somAbhAve tu bhUyo'vayavalipsayA susadRzAkSepe prasakte pUtIkopAdAnamAkSepasyA'pyapavAdakena vacanAntareNa [p.132] sidhyati | tatazca yadi mukhyazAstrapravRttyanantarameva saMskArazAstrANi pravarteran, tadA mukhyAbhAve nimitte satyAkSepavacanAntarAvagamyApUrvIyabhAvAnAM pratinidhInAmupadezataH saMskArAnvayo na sidhyediti tatsiddhyarthaM mukhyazAstrapravRttyanantarabhAvinAmAkSepavacanAntarANAmapi pravRttimapekSyaiva saMskArazAstrapravRttireSTavyA | tathA sati naimittikAnAmapi nityasAmAnavidhyamApAditaM nivArayituM na zakyate | vrIhizabdasyApUrvasAdhanalakSaNAparatve yathA naimittikazAstrANAM nityasAmAnavidhyadUSaNaM tathA mukhyArthavivakSApakSe pratinidhInAM mukhyasAmAnavidhyaM na syAt iti zaGkA | nanvevaM satyapUrvIyatvalakSaNApakSe naimittikAnAM nityasAmAnavidhyaM syAditi dUSaNamiva mukhyArthavivakSApakSe pratinidhInAM mukhyasAmAnavidhyaM na syAditi dUSaNamApatet, vrIhi-somAdividhAyakazAstrasthitAnAmiva tatsaMskAravidhAyakazAstrasthitAnAmapi vrIhisomAdizabdAnAM mukhyamAtraparatvena pratinidhisAdhAraNyAbhAvAditi cet, ucyate- vrIhi-somAdisaMskArANAM dvitIyAzrutyAdibhirvrIhyAdyarthatvenA'vagamyamAnAnAM prakaraNalabhya-tattadadhikArApUrvArthatvasya vrIhyAdidvAraiva nirvahaNIyatvAdapUrvasAdhanetaravrIhyAdInAM ca tatra dvAratvAyogAt | apUrvasAdhanavrIhyAdigaveSaNAyAM "vrIhibhiryajeta" "somena yajete"tyAdizAstrairvrIhyAdaya iva nyAyena prAptaparisaMkhyAyA udAharaNaM vRttikAramate | SoDazyuttara ityetat prAptaparisaMkhyodAharaNam | atra hi svArthabodhanaM nApekSitam, aGgirasAM dvirAtrasyottare 'hani SoDazigrahaniyamarUpasya doSatrayamanivAryam | ata eva parisaMkhyAsUtre razanAdAnamantraparisaMkhyAmaGgIkRtavatA'pi bhASyakAreNa "SoDazyuttara" ityatra vRttikArAbhimatA parisaMkhyA doSatrayApattyA dazame pratikSiptA | ityatra [p.148] atha trayastriMzazlokasyA'vatArikA | syAdetat- liGgAdevA'gneH prakRtyAzrayatvamapi prApnoti, "puruSArthasamAsatteH kAmyaM nityasya bAdhakami"ti nyAyena phalArthatayA balavatastasyottaravedibAdhakatvAt, puruSArthaprayuktA hi sarvasya pravRttirataH kAmyo'gniH phalasya pratyAsannaH zIghraM prayujyate, uttaravedistu kratvarthA'vilambena | atazcotkaTapravRttiviSayaH sa tasyA bAdhakaH | sAmAnya-vizeSanyAyena kAmyabalavattvasya zaGkAsamAdhAnAbhyAM pratipAdanam | na ca camasa-godohanavadekaprakaraNagatayoreva nityakAmyayoH kAmyabalavattvanyAyaH, prakaraNAmnAtAnArabhyAdhItayostu tayornityamapi prakaraNAmnAtameva balavat, anArabhyAdhItasya vikRtiSvapi sAvakAzatvena durbalatvAditi vAcyam | sAmAnyata iSTyadhikRtamAtraviSaye camaso viziSya pazukAmaviSaye godohanamiti sAmAnyavizeSanyAyena pazukAmAtiriktaviSaye camasaH sAvakAzaH, godohanaM niravakAzamiti sAvakAzaniravakAzanyAyena ca balavato godohanasyAsaGkIrNakAmyabalavattvanyAyAnudAharaNatayA sAmAnyavizeSabhAvAdinyAyAviSayasyA'gnyAdereva sakalamImAMsakaprasiddhakAmyabalavattvanyAyodAharaNataucityAt | [p.149] api ca tenAgniSTomasaMsthAvatyeva hi tasminnagniH prApyate, na tu saMsthAntaravatyapi | yathA "agravatIM bRhatpRSThasye"ti vAkyenAgravatI bRhatsAmnyeva tasminprApyate, na tu rathantarasAmnyapi tatazcokthyasaMsthasya, atirAtrasaMsthasya ca jyotiSTomasyA'GgatvenAprApto'gnirukthyAtirAtravAkyAbhyAM vidhIyate | ukthyAtirAtrazabdayorukthyAtirAtrasaMsthavikRtisAdhAraNye'pyagniSTomavAkyoktanyAyena teSu prakRti-vikRtisAdhAraNAgniSTomAdivAkyairevA'gneraupadezikatvasya, [p.152] tadadhInaguNakAmaprAptezca sampannatayA teSAM guNakAmaprAptyarthAgnividhirUpatvasya vaktumazakyatvAt | na ca teSu "AmnAtaM parisaMkhyArtham" iti sUtrAvalambanena | kiM tarhi dvirAtrAdivAkyAni kurvantu? agniSTomAdivAkyaiH sarveSvahargaNeSu prAptasyA'gnerdvirAtrAdyatiriktebhyaH pratikSeparUpAM parisaMkhyAM kurvantu teSAM prakRti-vikRtiparatvAbhyupagamAdupadezata eva dvirAtrAdiSvaharantareSvapi prAptirastIti prAptaparisaMkhyAyAM traidoSyaM syAditi cet - [p.153] pUrvoktazaMkAnirAsena stAvakatvopapAdanam | tarhyatra dvirAtrAdivAkyAni samanantaravidhInAM stutIH kurvantu- "evamete'gniSTomAdiSvagnividhayaH prazastA yatteSvagnividhAnamAtreNa dvirAtrAdyahargaNeSvapyagnirlabhyata" iti | itthameva hyaGgirasAM dvirAtre "SoDazyuttara" iti vacanam "uttare'han dvirAtrasya gRhyata" iti dvirAtrasAmAnyavacanaprAptasya SoDazino dvirAtrAntarebhyaH parisaMkhyArthamiti vRttikAramate prAptaparisaMkhyArthatvena [p.155] atha paJcatriMzazlokasyAvatArikA kiM taditi cettatrAha-
agniSTomAdizabdAH prakRtivikRtisaMsparzinassantu nAma prApyatvAmnAtasaMsthAnprathamamatha na te yAntyanAmnAtasaMsthAn | tattattyaktadvirAtraprabhRtiSu vidhayazcodakaprAptamagni zyenAkRtyAdilabdhyai vidadhata iti teSvakSatAvyAptateti || 35 || [p.156] pUrvazlokoktarItyA saGkoce kAraNAbhAvAdime'gniSTomAdizabdAH prakRtivikRtigAminaH santu nAma | ata eva prakRtisAmAnavidhyAtparisaMkhyAtvamanupapannamiti tantraratnavacanamapyagniSTomAdivAkyatrayAbhiprAyeNa yujyate, tathApi vikRtirUpAn sarvAnagniSTomAdInna spRzanti, kiM tvagniSTomAdisaMsthAvataH kratuvizeSAnanvicchanto
yeSu kratuSu pratyakSazrutyAmnAtAstattatsaMsthAH santi, teSu zIghrabuddhyupArUDheSu prathamamavataranti, tAvatA nairAkAGkSyAt, zabdAnAM viramya vyApArAbhAvAcca | tadanantaraM vilambitopasthitikeSu codakaprApyasaMstheSu kratuSu nA'vataranti parNamayAdivAkyasthitajuhvAdizabda iva codakaprAptAsu vaikRtajuhvAdiSu "upAMzu paurNamAsyAM yajet" iti vAkyAbhAve darzapUrNamAsAdhikAravAkyasthapUrNamAsapadamivA'ntarAlavidhisAmarthyalabhyapaurNamAsIkAle upAMzuyAje | AhavanIyavAkyagatajuhoterapi "agnayazca svakAlatvAd" (3|7|20|39) ityadhikaraNe sarvavikRtihomavidhisAdhAraNyaM nA'GgIkRtam, kintu ye vikRtiSvAmnAtahomAdaya upadiSTAH, na tvatidiSTAH
tatsAdhAraNyamevA'GgIkRtam, tanmAtrasAdhAraNye hyatidiSTasAdhAraNya iva na viramya vyApAraprasaktirasti | ata evA'tidezaprApyeSu homeSvAhavanIyasyA'pyatidezenaiva prAptistadadhikaraNavArtike darzitA | "tatazcA'havanIyena sahaivA'syA'tidezanAt | prakRtau vA dviruktatvAd (3|6|2) ityayaM nizcayo bhavet" iti | evaJca prakRtAnAmagniSTomAdizabdAnAM pratyakSAmnAtatattatsaMsthAvanmAtraviSayatayA teSvevAgnerupadezataH pravRttiH, yeSu tu dvirAtrAdiSu pratyakSAmnAtasaMsthAvizeSarahiteSu codakAddvAdazAhikamadhyamadazarAtragatatattatsaMsthAvizeSalAbhaH | teSvagniSTomAdivAkyarUpopadezaparityakteSvatidezAdevAgneH prAptiriti, teSu guNakAmA na
prApnuyuH atasteSu sAmAnavidhyena guNakAmaprAptyarthamagnividhiparANi dvirAtrAdivAkyAnIti, teSvavyAptikathanaM yuktamityetAvadiha vaktuM zakyam | iti paJcatriMzazlokasya vyAkhyA
atha SaTtriMzazlokasyAvatArikA nanvevameva samAdhAnamucyatAM ko doSa iti cettatrA'ha-
tatraitaddUSaNaM syAnna guNaphalavidhiSvatra vA'nyatra vA'gniH prAkkiJcitkratvapUrvAnvayamupagatavAnAzrayo'trAnuvAdyaH | kintvagniSTomamukhyakratusamadhigamAtprAganArabhyavAde [p.157] zuddho'gnistasya naiva kvacidapi vikRtAvAzrayatve'sti bhedaH || 36 || syAdidamuttaram-yadyagnyAzritaguNaphalavidhiSu
atraivA'gniprakaraNe'nyeSu vA kratuprakaraNeSu kiMcitkratvapUrvAnvayitvena siddho'gnirAzrayatvenA'nuvAdyo na syAt | tathA hi-yadi prAgevA'gnyAzritaguNaphalavidhipravRtteH keSu cit kratuprakaraNeSUpadezena ca tattatkratvapUrvAnvayo'gneH siddhaH syAt tadvyatirekeNa cA'tra guNaphalavidhiSvAzrayAnuvAdArthaM zuddhAgnisamarpakapurovAdo na syAt, tadAnImaupadezikajuhUnyAyena prathamaM buddhimavataratyaupadezike'gnAvAzrayatvenA'nvite, caramamAtidezikasyA'gneranvayo na bhavediti tasyA'pi tadAzrayatvenAnvayArthamupadezaH saphalaH syAt | yadi vA tatraiva prakaraNe kiMcitkratvapUrvAnvayitvena siddha evA'gnirappraNayanavadguNaphalavidhyanvayI
syAt, tadA godohanavadagnyAzritaguNaphalavidhistatraiva kratau vyavatiSTheteti kratvantaragatAgnerapi tadAzrayatvasiddhyarthaM yatnAntaraM kartavyaM syAt |
dvirAtrAdivAkyAnAM guNakAmaprAptyarthaM nApekSeti nirUpaNam
nanvevamanArabhyavAdarUpe'gniprakaraNe'gniSTomAdivAkyaiH kratvanvayakaraNAt prAgeva "ya evaM vidvAnagniM cinute" ityutpannaH zuddho'gniH zyenAkRtyAdiguNaphalavidhiSvAzrayatvenA'nveti, somaprayogAdbahiH kevalasyA'gneranuSThAtumazakyatvAdagniSTomAdivAkyaiH tasya kratuSu saMyojanAcca | yatrA'gnistatraiva tadAzritA guNaphalavidhaya ityarthAdeva teSAmapUrvIyAgnisaMbandhaH sidhyati, arthAtsidhyaccApUrvIyasyA''
zrayatvaM yeSvagneraupadezikamapUrvIyatvam, yeSu cA'tidezAdhInaM teSu sarveSvapyavizeSeNa sidhyati | na hyatrAtidiSTasyA'gnerAzrayatve dArzapaurNamAsi[p.158]kAppraNayanAzritatvenA'vagatasya godohanasyeSTyantarAtidiSTapraNayanAzrayavaiguNyavadiha kiMcidvaiguNyamasti, etAdRgviSayAbhiprAyeNaivA'tidiSTAzrayaNenA'pi guNakAmapravRttiraGgIkRtA | avaiguNye tu bhavatu kAmasyA'pi pravRttiriti | tadabhiprAyeNaiva bhASyakAro'pyAha "godohane pravartamAne tadAzritaH kAmo'pi pravarttate Azraye pravartamAne tadAzritamapi pravartate" (8|1|14) iti | tasmAdatidiSTAgnyAzrayaNenA'pi guNakAmaprAptisambhavAnna tadarthaM dvirAtrAdivAkyApekSeti
bhAvaH || 36 || iti SaTtriMzazlokavyAkhyA
atha saptatriMzazlokasyAvatArikA
kA tarhi dvirAtrAdivAkyAnAM gatiH? stAvakataivetyuktamiti cenna | agniSTomokthyAtirAtravAkyasarUpatayA tadvidhAyakatayA pratIyamAnAnAM vyarthaprAyastutiparatvakalpanAyogAt | tasmAttadvidhitvanirvAhAnyathAnupapattyaivA'tidiSTAzrayaNena guNakAmA na pravartanta iti kalpayiSyAmaH | tathA sati hi guNaprAptyarthatA teSAM bhavedityanuzayAnaM pratyAha-
vAcastvagnerdvirAtraprabhRtiSu parisaJcakSatAmanyato'gnim teSvanyAGgAni yadvA niyamanamatha vA teSu kurvantu tasya | yeSvetAH kliSTagatyA guNaphalabhavanaprApaNArthA bhaveyuH SoDazyartheSviva syAttadapi vidhiphalaM
teSvavazyaM tathA'pi || 37 ||
dvirAtrAdivAkyAnAM vyarthaprAyaM stAvakatvamiti manvAnaM prati parisaMkhyeti kathanam
yadi dvirAtrAdivAkyAnAM kena citprakAreNa vidhAyakatvaM vAcyamiti nirbandhaH tadA tAnyahargaNAntarebhyo'gneH parisaMkhyAM kurvantu | yadi prAptaparisaMkhyA tridoSA, tadapekSayA vyarthaprAyA stutireva varamityuktamiti tatrA'paritoSastarhi dvirAtrAdiSveva gRhamedhIyAjyabhAgavidhinyAyena codakaprAptAGgAntaraparisaMkhyAM kurvantu | yadi ca [p.159] dvirAtrAdiSvatidezaprAptAGgasadbhAve'pi liGgAni santIti tadvirodhAttatrApyaparitoSastarhi dvirAtrAdiSUttaravedyAH
pakSaprAptAvagnerniyamanaM kurvantu, dvirAtrAdicodakaparyAlocanAlabhyottaravediprAptyapekSAgniniyamApekSayA'gniprakaraNAmnAtadAzritaguNakAmaprAptirbuddhisaMnihiteti tAdarthyameva dvirAtrAdivAkyAnAM kalpayitumucitamiti prathamazlokavyAkhyAne pratipAditamiti cedidamapi tatra SoDazidvirAtrAdivacanamudAhRtya pratipAditameva-sannihitaphalAlAbhe prakaraNAntarasthacodakAdhInaprAptisApekSo niyamo'pi phalaM bhavitumarhatIti | iha cA'nyataH sambhavantI guNakAmaprAptiH phalaM na bhavatItyuktameva| yadyapi kliSTagatyA dvirAtrAdivAkyAnAM guNakAmaprAptyarthatvaM samarthyeta, tathApyagnerniyamanamapi phalaM sambhavanna nivArayituM zakyam | na hyekasya
vAkyasya prAptiniyamaparisaMkhyAsvekameva phalamiti niyamaH, anekaphalasaGkarasya tatra-tatra vAkye bahuzaH prAgudAhRtatvAt | anekaphalasAMkaryasya siddhAntasammatatve zAstradIpikA-tantraratnasUtramUlakatvenodAharaNAntarapradarzanam
SoDazigrahaviSayeSvanekaphalasaGkaraH kiJca dvirAtrAdivAkyeSveva siddhAntAbhimato dRzyate | tathA hi-"uttare'handvirAtrasya gRhyate" ityAdivAkyAni tatprAcInavAkyasaGkIrtitaM brAhmaNatvarAjanyatvarUpanimittAbhAve'pi SoDazinaH prApaNArthAnIti tAvat zAstradIpikAkRtoktam | tenaiva "uttare'hannitivAkyam" prakRtyatirAtre vikalpitasya SoDazinaH tathaiva dvirAtrasyottare'hani prAptau tanniyamArthamiti
tantraratne pratipAditam | sUtrakRtA tu "SoDazI vaikRtatvaM tatra kRtsnavidhAnAt" (10|5|9|34) ityadhikaraNe uttare'hannityAdivAkyAni parisaMkhyArthAnIti pratipAditam, tatra hi SoDazI prakRtau jyotiSTome na grAhyaH, tasya "madhyame'hani" ityAdivAkyaiH kArtsnyena vikRtisambandhitvAvagamAttadanusAreNA'gniSTomokthyAtirAtrazabdAnAM vikRtyagniSTomAdiparatvaucityAt | anyathA prakRtyagniSTomAdiSu satastasyA'tidezAdvikRtiSu prAptisambhavena madhyamAdivAkyavaiyarthyaprasaGgAditi pUrvapakSaM prApayya, jyotiSTomaprakaraNagatAnAM SoDaziviSayAgniSTomAdivAkyAnAM dvAdazopasattAdivAkyavattadananvayipadarahitAnAM prakaraNAdutkarSAyogAt
prakRtAvapi SoDazI grAhya [p.160] iti siddhAntite, kimarthAni tarhi madhyamAdivAkyAnItyAkAMkSAyAM "AmnAtaM parisaMkhyArtham" iti sUtreNA'tirAtrokthyAgniSTomasaMstheSvahargaNAnAmaharantareSvapi brAhmaNarAjanyayoH SoDazinaH prAptau tatparisaMkhyArthAnItyuktaM phalaM pratyAkhyAtavyam | tasmAt SoDaziviSayadvirAtrAdivAkyeSvivA'gniviSayadvirAtrAdivAkyeSvapyanivAryaH phalAntareNa saMkaraH | ataH phalAntareNa teSAM vidhitvanirvAhAdatidiSTAzrayaNena guNakAmA na pravartanta iti paribhASA nAzrayaNIyeti kathamapi dvirAtrAdivAkyeSvavyAptiravakAzaM na labhata iti bhAvaH | iti saptatriMzazlokavyAkhyA

[p.160] guNaphalasambandhavidhayA'vyAptiparihAravarNanam

evaM dvirAtrAdivAkyeSvavyApteranavakAzatvaM saprapaJcamupapAditam | "pazukAma ukthyaM gRhNIyAt" "SoDazinA vIryakAmaH stuvIta" "atirAtreNa prajAkAmaM yAjayet" iti vAkyAni pazvAdiphalArthaM jyotiSTomAzritokthyAdisaMsthArUpaguNavidhAyakAnIti matamAzritya ukthyAtirAtravAkyayorapyavyApteranavakAzatvaM prAgupapAditam |

ye tu manyante-pazukAmAdivAkyAni pazvAdyarthamukthyAdyadhikAvRttiyuktaprakRtasomayAgavidhAyakAni na tu tadAzritokthyAdisaMsthAmAtravidhAyakAni | yatra hi lokataH kratorguNAnvayo labhyate, tatraiva phalAya
guNamAtrasya vidhiryujyate | yathA godohanAdeH | yatra tu zAstrAdRte na tatsiddhiH tatra na sa yujyate, guNAzrayasaMbandhe, guNaphalasaMbandhe ca zAstravyApArAyogAt, kintu tatra guNaviziSTakratoreva phalAya vidhAnaM vAcyam | ata eva "dAkSAyaNayajJena svargakAmo yajeta" (tai.saM. 2|5|5) ityAdiSvapyAvRttiguNaviziSTasya kratoreva phalAya vidhAnamabhyupagamyate | evaM ca phalakAmanAnimittakatadadhikAvRttiyuktokthyAdisaMsthaH somayAgo [p.161] nityavadAmnAtatadAvRttyayuktasyA'gniSTomasaMsthasya "vasante vasante jyotiSA yajeta" "jyotiSTomena svargakAmo yajeta" (Apa zrau. sU.) iti
vidhidvayaprAptanityakAmyobhayAvasthasya somayAgasya vikAra iti tatra dIkSaNIyAderaGgakalApasyA'tidezenaiva prAptirna tUpadezena | nityavadAmnAtadIkSaNIyAdyupadezasya zIghropasthitikanityavadAmnAtAgniSTomasaMsthanityakAmyakratuprayogAnvayena caritArthasya vilambitopasthitakanaimittikokthyAdigrahA''kSepyAdhikAvRttiyuktaprayogAntarAnvayakalpane dviruktiprasaGgAt-iti, tanmatAzrayaNenokthyAtirAtrAgnivAkyayoratidezataH sambhavatprAptikAgnividhirUpayoravyAptirvaktuM zakyata ityata Aha-

syAtsArvakAmyoktyuditaphalabhidAsAdhikAyAM prakRtyAm ukthyAdInAM vyavasthA prakaraNapaThanAtsarvatastatprasaktau ||38 ||
"pazukAma ukthyaM gRhNIyAt" ityAdivAkyeSUkthyAdizabdavAcyAmukthyAdistotraiH kratusamAptirUpokthyAdisaMsthAmatilaGghyokthyAdipadalakSyasyokthyAdisaMsthAviziSTasya kratoH pazvAdiphalasaGgatiraGgIkriyate cet, pazukAmAdivAkyAni kratuphalavidhayo na bhaveyuH | "sarvebhyaH kAmebhyaH" iti vAkyAntareNaiva kratoH pazvAdisakalaphalAnvayasiddheH | tatazcokthyAdisaMsthe kratau phalArthasya vidhyantarasyA'bhAvAttadvidhividheya ukthyAdyadhikAvRttiyuktaH kraturagniSTomasaMsthasya kratorvikRtirityevaM na sidhyedeva | kiM tarhi
pazukAmAdivAkyaiH sidhyediti cet, ucyate-vasantAdivAkyena jIvadadhikAratayA sarvakAmavAkyena tattatphalakAmAdhikAratayA cA'vagate prakRtibhUte jyotiSTome tatprakaraNotpannAnAmukthyAdInAmavizeSeNa saMbandhe prApte pazukAmAdivAkyairukthyasya pazuphalArthe tasminsaMbandhaH SoDazino vIryaphalArtha ityAdirUpA vyavasthA paraM tairvAkyaiH sidhyati | yathA vRSTyAdiphalatrayArthavihitasaubharapaThitAnAM trayANAmapi hISItyevamAdInAM nidhanAnAmavizeSeNa tatsaMbandhe prApte "hISiti vRSTikAmAya nidhanaM kuryAt" "UrgityannAdyakAmAya" "U iti svargakAmAya" ityAdivAkyairvRSTyarthe hISiti nidhanamityAdivyavasthA sidhyati, evaM [p.162]

vastutastu teSAM kratusaGgatisvIkAra evA'yuktaH, saMsthAvizeSaviziSTeSu kratuSUkthyAdizabdaprayogasya "vizeSyaM nAbhidhA gacchet kSINazaktirvizeSeNa" itinyAyena

"saMsthAstu samAnavidhAnAH prakaraNAvizeSAt" (3|6|16|41) iti saMsthAdhikaraNe "kiM sarvasaMsthaM jyotiSTomaM prakRtya dIkSaNIyAdayo dharmA
uktAH, utAgniSTomasaMsthamabhipretya" (zA. bhA. pR. 1060) ityAdibhASyavacanaM tu jyotiSTomasyaivA'gniSTomasaMsthasyokthyAdisaMsthasya ca sAmAnavidhyAsAmAnavidhyavicAraparaM na grAhyam, kintu jyotiSTomena tadAzritAnAmukthyAdisaMsthAnAM pRthak phalavattvenetikartavyatApekSiNInAM sAmAnavidhyAsAmAnavidhyavicAraparaM netavyam | ata eva "yatra yatra guNakAmo bhavati tatra tatra kriyAyAM sAdhyamAnAyAM nAnyathA, [p.163] tatretikartavyatAyA antikamupanipatati sA sAdhanasya vA sAdhyasya veti sandihyamAnA sAdhyasya bhavitumarhati, nA'sau sAdhyasyA'bhavantI sAdhanena saMbadhyate" (zA0bhA0 1069-70) iti

athaikonacatvAriMzazlokasyAvatArikA
athAkSepAntarANyapi yathA sambhavamAkSipyante | yaduktam-sAptadazyavAkyavadabhyudayArthatvAGgIkAreNa svargakAmAdivAkyeSvapUrvavidhilakSaNAvyAptyuddhAre, tasya niyamaparisaMkhyAvidhyorativyAptiH syAditi, tatra sAptadazyavAkyadRSTAntakathanamayuktamityAkSipati-
tAsu syAtsAptadazyaM prakaraNarahitaM yAsu na zrUyate tattAsvasya syAdvikalpo vikRtiSu kathite codakAtpAJcadazye | satyevaM mitravindAdiSu niyamavidhistasya bhUyo vidhissyAt sArthakye sambhavatyabhyudayaphalakatA
kevalaM naiva kalpyA ||39 || [p.164]
anArabhyAdhItasya sAptadazyasyAtidezakena pAJcadazyavikalpaH, mitravindAdiSu punaH sAptadazyavidhAnaM vikalpApavAdarUpaM sAptadazyaniyamArthamiti svamatasyopapAdanam
anArabhyAdhItasAptadazyavAkyasyA'pi prAkaraNikasAptadazyavAkyavanmitravindAdimAtrazaraNatve syAdevamagatyAbhyudayArthatvakalpanam | tadevA'siddham, tasya pravRttivizeSakaratvaparyavasAyigatyantarasambhavAt | tathA hi-yAsu sauryAdivikRtiSu sAptadazyaM na zrUyate, tAsvanArabhyavAdaprAptasAptadazyasyA'tidezaprAptena pAJcadazyena vikalpaH prApnotu; mitravindAdiSu punaH sAptadazyavidhAnaM vikalpApavAdarUpaM sAptadazyaniyamArthamastu | evaM sarvasyA'pi
sAptadazyavAkyasya pravRttivizeSakaratvasambhave kimityanyataH sidhyatyarthe kevalAbhyudayArthavidhitvamabhyupagamyate |
anArabhyAdhItasAptadazyasya "na tau pazau karoti" iti dRSTAntena vikalpadvArA kevalAdRSTArthatvaM sAptadazyodAharaNasya vighaTanopasaMhAraH
nanvanArabhyAdhItasAptadazyasya sauryAdigAmitve sauryAdiSvatidezAtpAJcadazyaM na prApnuyAt, upadezenA'tidezabAdhAditi cet, satyamanyatra tathaiva | iha tu sauryAdiSu sAptadazyaM pAJcadazyAtidezabAdhanena svayaM niyatatvaM prApnoti cenmitravindAdiSu punaH sAptadazyavidhAnaM vyarthaM syAt | tathaiva mitravindAdiSvapi sAptadazyaniyamalAbhopapatteH | ato'nArabhyAdhItaM sAptadazyavAkyaM svasya
mitravindAdigatau svavaiyarthyaM sauryAdigatau pAJcadazyAtidezabAdhena prAkaraNikasAptadazyavAkyavaiyarthyaM cAlocya, sauryAdikaM gatvA tatra pAJcadazyAtidezaJca soDhvA, tena saha vikalpamanumanyate, yathA "na tau pazau karoti" iti pazAvAjyabhAgapratiSedhastatra codakAdAjyabhAgayoraprAptAvaprasaktapratiSedhatayA svavaiyarthyaM codakAttayoH prAptau sarvathA tayoH pratiSedhe ca tatprApakasya codakasyA'prAmANyApattiM cAlocyAjyabhAgAtidezaM soDhvA tadanuSThAnena saha vikalpamaGgIkaroti | evaM hi tatra codakaniSedhazAstrayorivAtrA'pi prAkaraNikAprAkaraNikasAptadazyavAkyayoH pravRttivizeSakaratayA sAphalyaM bhavati | tasmAt kevalAdRSTArthatve
sAptadazyavAkyodAharaNamayuktamiti bhAvaH || 39 ||
ityekonacatvAriMzazlokasya vyAkhyA

[p.165] atha catvAriMzazlokasya vyAkhyA
yaduktamapUrvavidhilakSaNe sarvoddezyAnuvRttiraprApterAtyantikatvamiti pakSe "tredhA taNDulAnvibhajet" iti vidhAvavyAptiH, tatra dArzikahaviSAM pUrvadevatAbhyo'panayasya, yakSyamANadevatAbhyo vibhAgasya vA vidhau teSAM madhye taNDuladadhizRteSu devatApanayavibhAgayorarthasiddhatvenopAMzuyAjAjyamAtre vidhezcAritArthyasya vaktavyatvAditi | tadAkSipati-
nAstyevopAMzuyAjo dadhizRtasahitaH paurNamAsyAM vidhAnAt tadvazyaM darzatantre paThitamapi RgAmnAtayAjyAnuvAkyam | kRtvAcintaiva tasya kvacidadhikaraNe dArzikeSvastitoktiH tattredhA taNDulAnityanuvadanamatastatra nA'vyAptirasti ||40||
dadhizRtavatyAmamAvAsyAyAmupAMzuyAjo nAstyeva "upAMzu paurNamAsyAM yajet" iti paurNamAsyAM tadvidhAnAt | na ca vAcyaM bahRcabrAhmaNe amAvAsyAprakaraNe upAMzuyAjayAjyAnuvAkyAyugalapAThAttasyAmapi so'stIti, pradhAnasyopAMzuyAjasya paurNamAsIkAlatve tadaGgatayA tatparatantrasya mantrasyA'nyatra paThitasyA'pi tadgAmitvAt | ayaJcArthaH sUtrakRtaiva dazamAdhyAye "amAvAsyAyAmupAMzuyAjaH syAdupAMzuyAjamantarA yajatItyavizeSazravaNAd" iti pUrvapakSaM kRtvA "api vA paurNamAsyAM syAtpradhAnazabdasaMyogAnmantro yathA pradhAnaM syAt" (10|8|27|54) iti siddhAntasUtreNa spaSTIkRtaH | prapaJcitaM caitaccaturthazlokavyAkhyAne |
nApyetacchaGkanIyamamAvAsyAyAmupAMzuyAjamaGgIkRtyA'pi upAMzuyAjo vacanAdyathAprakRtItyAdyadhikaraNapravRttirdRzyate iti, tasya nyAyavyutpAdanArthaM kRtvAcintArUpatayA netavyatvAt, anyathA "paurNamAsyAM yajet" ityAhatya paurNamAsIkAlatvavidhAyakazrutivyAkopaprasaGgAt | udAhRtazrutyavalambanAnupadodAhRtadAzamikAdhikaraNavirodhaprasaGgAcca | tasmAt "tredhA taNDulAnvibhajed" ityayaM vakSyamANasya taNDulAnAM madhyamAdibhAvena vibhAgasyA'nuvAda ityeva yuktam | AkaragrantheSUktarItyA'nuvAdatvaparihArAya dArzikahaviHsAmAnye lakSaNAmaGgIkRtya pUrvadevatApanayasya [p.166] vakSyamANadevatAbhyo vibhAgasya vA vidheyatvAbhyupagame'pi tasya
taNDuladadhizRteSvarthasiddhatvenopAMzuyAjAjyasyA'bhAvena ca vidheyAlAbhenA'nuvAdatvAparihArAcca | tasmAt "tredhA taNDulAn" iti vidhau sarvoddezyAnuvRttaprAptyabhAvenA'vyAptivarNanamayuktamiti bhAvaH ||40||
iti catvAriMzazlokasya vyAkhyA
athaikacatvAriMzazlokasyAvatArikA
evamabhyudayeSTivAkye dArzikahaviSAM pUrvadevatAbhyo'panayasyA'pekSAmaGgIkRtya, tasyA'rthasiddhatvAdvidhivaiyarthyamuktam | idAnIM tadapekSaiva nA'stIti nitarAM tasya vaiyarthyamAha-
pratyekaM dravyayugme na paTu ghaTayituM devatAM vAkyamekaM sArUpyasyA'pi bhaGgaH pazuphalavacasA kevalaM tadvidhAne | tasmAttAvadviziSTAnvayini sati vidhAvArtivAkyoktarItyA vaktavyo
devatAnAmapanayanavidhiH kalpanaM kvopayuktam ||41||
bhavediha dArzikahaviSAM pUrvadevatApanayApekSA, yadi teSAM devatAntarasaMyojanaparANi madhyamAdivAkyAni syuH, tadevAsiddham | tathAtve vAkyabhedaH prasajyate | tathA hi-madhyamAdivAkyAnAM devatAntarasaMyojanaparatve dravyeSvagnyAdidevatAvidhAnam, devatAsu dAtRtvAdiguNavidhAnaJceti triSvapi vAkyeSu tAvadeko vAkyabhedaH, dadhi-zRtavAkyayoH sthaviSThAnAM zRtaM cAdhikaraNaM vidheyamityaparo vAkyabhedaH, tathA sthaviSThAnAmaNiSThAnAM carubhAvo'pi vidheya ityanyo vAkyabhedaH | syAdetat-naite vAkyabhedAH prasaranti | tathA hi-dAtRpradAtRzipiviSTazabdAnAM zrutyaivA'gnyAdipadasAmAnAdhikaraNyAt padadvayapratipAditaM
viziSTadevatAkArakamekameva vidheyam | na tu devatAstadguNAzca pratyekaM vidheyAH | abhyudayeSTiprakaraNe "saha [p.167] zrapayati" iti vacanamasti | tadetaddevataikyAddadhi-sthaviSThayoH zRtANiSThayozca sahapAkaviSayam | tatazca sthaviSThAnAmaNiSThAnAJca taNDulAnAmalpatvAddadhizRtayorbhUyastvAcca taNDulAnAM carutvaM dadhizRtayoradhikaraNatvaM cA'rthAtprApnoti | arthaprAptaJca tatsarvamanUdyate, tattadguNaviziSTamekadevatAkArakaM vidhIyate, madhyamAnAM taNDulAnAmagnirdAtA devatA, sthaviSThAnAM dadhnazcendraH pradAtA | aNiSThAnAM zRtasya ca viSNuH zipiviSTa iti | ato naivaM vAkyabhedAvakAza iti cet-
ekaM sandhitsato'paraM pracyavata iti nyAyena pUrvoktarItyA vAkyabhedaparihAre'pi prakArAntareNa vAkyabhedakathanam
satyamitthaM saha zrapaNe vAkyAntaramaGgIkRtya kalpane uktaprakAreNa vAkyabhedo nA'stIti, tathA'pi prakArAntareNa vAkyabhedaH syAdeva | dadhivAkye dadhni sthaviSTheSu zRtavAkye zRte kSodiSTheSu ca pratyekaM devatAsaMyojanAt | iyAMstu bhedaH-saptamyarthavivakSayA'dhikaraNatvavidhipakSe sthaviSTheSu devatA, praNItakArye ca dadhividheyamiti, kSodiSTheSu devatA praNItakArye zRtaM ca vidheyamiti ca vidheyabhedAdvAkyabhedaH | dvitIyapakSe sthaviSTheSu dadhni ca, kSodiSTheSu zRte ca devatAvidheyetyuddezyabhedAdvAkyabheda ityubhayatrA'pyaviziSTo
vidhyAvRttilakSaNo vAkyabhedaH |
devatAntarasaMyojanapakSe pazukAmeSTigatamadhyamAdivAkyasArUpyabhaGgavarNanam
api cA'tra madhyamAdivAkyeSu dArzikahaviSAM devatAntarasaMyojanavidhiriti pakSe pazukAmArthamadhyamAdivAkyasArUpyabhaGgaH prasajyeta | evaM hi kAmyeSTikANDe zrUyate-"yaH pazukAmaH syAtso'mAvAsyAmiSTvA vatsAnapAkuryAt, ye madhyamAH syustAnagnaye dAtre puroDAzamaSTAkapAlaM kuryAt, ye sthaviSThAstAnindrAya pradAtre dadhaMzcaruM ye'NiSThAstAnviSNave zipiviSTAya zRte carum" iti | tatra pazukAmeSTividhau madhyamAdivAkyeSu dravyadevatAviziSTaikarmavidhiraGgIkRto (9|4|11) navame, tathAtrApi tathAbhUtakarmavidhyaGgIkAre |
tasmAdihA'pyavAkyabhedAya, pazukAmeSTivAkyasArUpyasAmaJjasyAya ca tattaddravyavizeSaviziSTaikarmavidhirevA'GgIkartuM yuktaH | [p.168] nanu tatra prakRtakarmapratyabhijJAnAbhAvAdagatyA viziSTavidhiraGgIkRtaH, iha prakRte dArze karmaNi pratyabhijJAyamAne naimittikadevatAntaramAtravidhAnena lAghave sambhavati viziSTavidhigauravamaGgIkartuM na yuktamiti cenna, ArtivAkyanyAyena prakRtakarmapratyabhijJAnamanAdRtyA'pyavAkyabhedAya, viziSTaikarmAntaravidherevAGgIkartumucitatvAt |
havirArtivAkye pUrvottarapakSAbhyAM karmAntarapratipAdanam
tathA hi-darzaprakaraNa eva zrUyate "yasyobhayaM havirArtimArcched aindraM paJcazarAvamodanaM nirvapet" iti vAkye
Artadohapratinidhitvena paJcazarAvadravyamAtraM karmaNaH sAdhanaM vidhIyate, na dravyadevatAviziSTaikarmAntaram, gauravAditi SaSThe "paJcazarAvastu dravyazruteH pratinidhiH syAt" iti (6|4|9|27) sUtreNa pUrvapakSaM kRtvA siddhAntitam-prApte karmaNi paJcazarAvadravyam, indro devateti dvayaM vidhAtuM na zakyate, vAkyabhedaprasaGgAt | na sAnnAyyadevatAyA indrasya sthAnApattyA prAptisambhavAdindrazabdo'nuvAda iti vAcyam | sAnnAyye mahendrasyA'pi kadA citsambhavenendrasya nityaprAptyabhAvAt | tasmAnnityatayendrasyA'pi vidheyatvAdvAkyabhedAya dravyadevatAviziSTadohArtinimittakakarmAntaravidhirevAGgIkartavyaH-iti |
evaJca dravyArtyA prakRtasya sAnnAyyayAgasya dravyApekSAyAM sphuTAyAM "yadi satrAya dIkSitAnAM sAmyuttiSThAsetsomamapabhajya vizvajitA yajeta" iti vAkye saMkalpamadhye satraM tyaktvottiSThAsoH somakrayAtpUrvaM pazcAccA'vizeSeNa prAyazcittavidhAyakamiti "sAmyutthAne vizvajitkrIte vibhAgasaMyogAt" (6|7|25) ityadhikaraNe nirNIte somavibhAgavacanasya somakrayAnantarakAlottiSThAsuyajamAnakartRkasvakIyasomAMzavibhAgAnuvAdakatvavadaindrayAjiyajamAnoddeSTavyadevatAnuvAdakatvamiti pakSaprAptAnuvAdatvAbhyupagamena kathaM cidvAkyabhedasya parihartuM zakyatve'pi aindrapadasya vidheyasamarpakatve sambhavati, nirarthakapAkSikAnuvAdatvaM na
kalpanIyamiti nyAyamAtramavalambyAvAkyabhedAya karmAntaravidhAvaGgIkRte kimu vaktavyamabhyudayeSTivAkye karmAntaravidhiraGgIkartavya iti | na hi tatrendrasaMbandhasyevAtra yatkiJcidravyadevatAsaMbandhasya pakSe prAptirasti | yena dadhivAkye zRtavAkye ca pratyekaM [p.169] devatAsaMbandhakRto vAkyabhedaH kathaJcidapi parihriyeta, na vA tatra dravyamAtravidhipakSotthApakaprakRtakarmagatadravyAkAGkSAnimittadravyApacArAnukIrtanavadiha devatAmAtravidhipakSotthApakadevatAkAGkSAnimittadevatApacArAnukIrtanamasti | na ca tredhA taNDulAniti vAkyasya devatApanayavidhiparatvaM spaSTam, yena

[p.170] guNavidhisvIkAreNa karmAntaravidhinirAse tulyanyAyatayA''rtivAkye'pi guNavidhizaGkA
nanu tarhi guNavidhirevAstu; yasya havirniruptamityAdyadhikArivizeSaNAdakAlopakrAntaprakRtakarmapratyabhijJAne jAgrati tatra guNamAtravidhAnamatilaGghya, guNaviziSTaikarmAntaravidhikalpanAyogAt | jJAte caivaM pratyabhijJAbalAdguNavidhitAtparye tadanurodhenA''patannanekadravyoddezena devatAguNavidhAnaprayukto vAkyabhedo na doSa iti cet | evaM tarhyArtivAkye'pyevameva guNamAtravidhiH syAt, tatrA'pi "yasyobhayaM haviH" ityadhikArivizeSaNataH sAnnAyyahaviSkaprakRtakarmapratyabhijJAnAt | yattu-havirnAze [p.171] karmaNa eva nAzAttatra prakRtakarmapratyabhijJA nA'stIti | tattuccham, upAttahavirnAze havirantarotpAdanena karmAnuSThAnasya "zeSAdavadAnanAze syAttadarthatvAt" (6|4|1|1) ityadhikaraNe vyavasthApitatvena "yasya sarvANi havIMSi nazyeyurduSyeyurapahareyurvA, Ajyena tA devatAH parisaMkhyAya yajeta" (Apa.zrau.sU.) iti zravaNena ca karmalopAprasakterakAlakRtamiti prasiddheratrApyakAlopakrAntakarmAbhAvena pratyabhijJAnAbhAvasya vaktuM zakyatvAt | tasmAdArtivAkyeSvapi karmAntaravidhireva vaktavya iti nAsti tatra dArzikahaviSAM pUrvadevatApanayasyopayogaH, karmAntaravidhau taNDulAdInAmupAdeyatvena prakRtagrahaNe kAraNAbhAvAt | ye madhyamA iti yacchabdasya

ityekacatvAriMzazlokasya vyAkhyA

[p.174] atha dvicatvAriMzazlokasyAvatArikA
evaM tarhyabhyudayeSTeH karmAntaratve'pi taddevatAbhyaH prakRtahaviSA vibhajanavidhiH syAdeva, upAMzuyAjAjye tasyA'rthavattvAt | karmAntarAbhyudayeSTAvupAMzuyAjAbhAve'pi prakRtahavirantargatopAMzuyAjAjyasyA'bhyudayeSTidevatAbhyo vibhajanopapatterityAzaGkAmudbhAvya nirAkaroti-
tarhi syAddevatAbhyaH pravibhajanavidhistatra tadgAminIbhyo na syAdevaM hi sarvAsvapi dadhizRtayordevatAsvanvayaH syAt | tasminvAkye vidheyaM vibhajanamiha cedvakSyamANaprakAraM na syAdaupAMzuyAje haviSi taditi tatsarvathA vyarthameva ||42||
yadi prakRtahaviSAmavizeSeNA'bhyudayeSTidevatAbhyo vibhajanamabhimatam, tadA dadhizRtayorapi
sarvadevatAnvayaH syAt | uttaratra tayorviziSya saGkIrtanaM sthaviSThairaNiSThaizca taNDulaiH saha zrapaNaniyamArthaM syAt | atha vakSyamANaprakAreNa vibhajanavidhiH "tredhA taNDulAnvibhajed" iti vAkye'GgIkriyate; tathA ca dadhizRtayorvakSyamANadevatAvizeSavyavasthA na bhajyata iti matam, tadopAMzuyAjAjye vakSyamANo devatAnvayaprakAro nA'stIti vibhajanavAkyaM tadviSayatvenA'rthavanna bhavet | taNDuladadhizRteSu tu tadvAkyaireva tathA vibhajanaM siddhamiti sarvathA vyarthameva tredhA taNDulAniti vAkyam | atastaNDulazabdalakSaNAkleze'pi tadvaiyarthyAnapAyAttasya tathAzrutAnuvAdakatvameva yuktamiti, tatra vidhyabhAvAttadvidhyavyAptivarNanamapyasamaJjasamiti bhAvaH
||42||
iti dvicatvAriMzazlokasya vyAkhyA
atha trayazcatvAriMzazlokasyAvatArikA [p.175]
evamAkSipteSvapUrvavidhyAdInAM lakSaNeSu, udAharaNeSu ca lakSaNAni vyavasthApayituM niyamaparisaMkhyAlakSaNasaMgrAhyasandehapadAni nirdhArayanneva lakSaNavyavasthApanaM pratijAnIte-
niyamaparisaMkhyayorlakSaNavyavasthApanam
evaM prApte bhaNAmastrividhavidhijuSAM lakSaNAnAM vibhAgaM dRSTArtho yaH sa sarvo bhavati niyama ityabhyupetya vyavasthAm | arthAdvA zabdato vA svaviSayagalitAdevakArAnnaJo vA yaH syAtkasyApi bAdhaH sa bhavati parisaMkhyeti rItiM ca kRtvA ||43 ||
prAgagratAdividhInAM
dRSTArthAnAmaprAptaviSayatvAdapUrvavidhitvamaGgIkRtya, teSu lakSaNAvyAptiruktA | pUtIkAghAramantraniyamavidhyativyAptyudbhAvanena tatsamAnanyAyatayA sarveSAmapi dRSTArthAnAM niyamavidhInAM niyamavidhitvamapi sUcitam | tathA niSedharUpavidhiSvapUrvavidhilakSaNAvyAptyudbhAvanena "dIkSito na juhoti" ityAdInAmapUrvavidhitvaM sUcitam | tathA niSedhamAtrasya parisaMkhyAtvAbhyupagamo'pyasUci | tenA'tra niyamaparisaMkhyAlakSaNasaMgrAhyAsaMgrAhyasandehaprAptAvidamucyate |
niyamavidhilakSaNasya prAptaviSayatvAprAptaviSayatvAbhyAM dvaividhyanirUpaNam
aprAptaviSayaH prAptaviSayo vA yo vidhirdRSTArthaH, sa sarvo'pi niyamavidhirityabhyupagacchAmaH |
aprAptaviSaye'pi tasminniyamAdRSTAbhyupagamAvazyaMbhAvAt | tathA hi-dRSTArthAprAptavidhiviSayo dvividhaH-vidhiM vinA svataH prasiddhadRSTaprayojanajanakasAmarthyaH, vidhibalaprasAdhanIyatajjananasAmarthyazca | tatra "audumbaro yUpo bhavati" ityAdyaH | prakRtau yUpe pakSaprApto'pyudumbaraH khadirAdiniyamavidhinA nivartita iti, vikRtAvapi codakAt khadirAdereva prAptyA pratiprasavavidhiviSayanyAyena somApauSNapazAvaprAptaivodumbarajAtiraSTAzritvAdiviziSTayUpAkRtijanane svata eva prasiddhasAmarthyA ca | [p.176] uttarAghArazAkhAchedanamantrotkaraparidhyAdirdvitIyaH | vidhibalAdeva hi hiraNyagarbhe mantrasya gauNavRttikalpanayA indraprakAzane, "iSe tvA" iti mantrasya chinadmItyadhyAhAreNa zAkhAchedanaprakAzane, utkarasya pRthvagratAkaraNena vAjinapAtradhAraNe, paridheH sthaviSThagrahaNena pazuniyojane ca sAmarthyaM prasAdhanIyam | tatazca tadvaividhyAttadviSayo vidhirapi dvividhaH | evaM dvividhe'pyaprAptaphalake dRSTArthavidhau niyamAdRSTAbhyupagama AvazyakaH, codakaprAptakhadirAdinA'pi sambhavati yUpAdisvarUpamAtre, udumbarAdiniyamavidhAnasyAnarthakyAt | ata evAhuH-"dRSTArtheSvapi sarveSu niyamAdRSTamiSyate" iti (nyA0 20 aM0) | anyathA'vahananAdiniyamavidhAvapi niyamAdRSTakalpanaM na syAt | nAnopAyasAdhye dRSTaprayojane viziSTasya kasya cinniyamo'narthakaH, tasya kratvapUrvopayogitvamapi tajjanyaM dvAraM
vinA'nupapannam, kratvapUrvasya pradhAnAnuSThAnAnantarabhAvitvAditi etadeva hi teSu niyamAdRSTakalpanAyAM bIjam, tadihApi tulyam | niyamAdRSTAbhyupagame ca niyamavidhitvameva prApnoti, ata eva pUtIkavidheH pakSaprAptaviSayatvAbhAve'pi niyamavidhitvam | "sarve dravyavidhayo niyamavidhaya" ityAkAreNa sakalamImAMsakapravAdazca |
asminnarthe bhASyatantraratnAdInAmAnukUlyamiti pratipAdanam
api ca nAvamike patnIsaMyAjAdhikaraNe "patnIsaMyAjAntAnyahAni santiSThante" ityasya bhASyAdiSu yanniyamavidhitvamuktam, tadapyaprAptasyaiva patnIsaMyAjAntatvasya dRSTArthatAmAtreNa, na tu tasya pAkSikaprAptyA, tadasambhavAt | tathA hi-yadyapi dvAdazAnAmahnAM
sahaprayogavacanAtpUrvapUrvamahaH kasmiMzcitpadArthe'vasthApya, paraM paramupakramitavyam, tathA'pi patnIsaMyAjeSvavasthApanaM pakSe'pi na prApnoti, upAMzvAdihAriyojanaparyantaH somayAgo hyahaHzabdenocyate | yathoktaM tantraratne "jyotiSTome'haHzabdenopAMzvAdihAriyojanaparyantaH somayAgaH paricchinnaH | ihApi dvAdazAhe evaM draSTavyam" iti | tathA ca sahatvasiddhyarthaM hAriyojanAnantaramevA'vasthApanaM prApnoti, yadi tu "vasatIvarIparyantAni pUrvANi tantramanyakAlatvA[p.177]davabhRthAdInyuttarANi dIkSAvisargArthatvAd" iti sUtroktarItyA dvAdazAhe vasatIvarIparyantAnAM pUrveSAm,
avabhRthAdInAmuttareSAJcAGgAnAM tantratve'pi "bhedastu tadbhedAtkarmabhedaH prayoge syAtteSAM pradhAnazabdAt" (11|2|1|10) iti sUtroktanyAyena pradhAnairapRthakkAlAnyaGgAni tattatpradhAnakAle bhedenAnuSTheyAnIti tattatsutyAkAlInAnAmaiSTikapAzukakarmaNAM sAGgAnAM tatra tatrAhani bhedenAnuSThAnaM prApnuyAt | tathA kArtsnyena pAzukaiSTikaprayogasamAptyanantaramevA'vasthAnaM prApnoti | sarvathApi patnIsaMyAjAnantaramavasthAnasya na pAkSikI prAptirasti | tathA ca patnIsaMyAjAnto'gnISomIyaH santiSThata itivadaprAptavidhirevAyam | tathA'pi sahatvanirvAhakatayA, dRSTArthatAsattvAttAvanmAtreNa niyamavidhitvaM bhASyAdiSUktamiti samarthanIyam |
"tatrA'niyame prApte patnIsaMyAjAnte saMsthAtavyamiti niyamyata" iti bhASyaM ca hAriyojanAntatvAdereva prAptyA patnIsaMyAjAntaniyamasyAbhAve prApta ityevaM yojanIyam | "yasminkasmiMzcidaniyamenAvasthAne prApte patnIsaMyAjAntatA niyamyata" iti tantraratnamapi hAriyojanapracAre, pazvAdisamApanakarmaNi vA yasminkasmiMzcitpatnIsaMyAjAntaniyamaM vinA'vasthAne prApta iti yojanIyaM, na tu yathAzrutaM grAhyaM, patnIsaMyAjAntatAyA aniyamataH prAptyasambhavasyoktatvAt | parisaMkhyAlakSaNAkSepe tu yathAzrutabhASyAdyanurodhAdaniyamo'smAbhirupanyastaH, tasmAddRSTArthaH sarvo vidhirniyamavidhiriti yuktameva | na caivaM

parisaMkhyAvidhilakSaNam
tathA''rthAdvA svaviSayapracyutAllAkSaNikazabdAdvA, evakArAdvA, naJpadAdvA labhyo yasya kasyApi bhAvasya vA'bhAvasya vA yo bAdhaH, sa sarvo'pi parisaMkhyeti maryAdAM kurmahe | tatra "atihAyeDo barhiH prati samAnayate juhvAmaupabhRtam" ityatrArthAduparitanaprayAjadvaye jauhavAjyasya, prAcInaprayAjatraye aupabhRtAjyasya ca bAdhaH "dve AhavanIya" ityatra "yadAhavanIye juhoti" iti sAmAnyazAstraprAptervidhisvarUpaviSayapracyutAdaprAptaniSedharUpArthAntaralakSakAttasmAdeva zabdAdagnihotrAhutyantarAbhyAseSvAhavanIyabAdhaH | "atra hyevAvapanti" ityatraivakAreNa pavamAnAtiriktastotreSvAvApabAdhaH | "na tau pazau karoti" ityatra naJpadena pazAvAjyabhAgabAdhaH | [p.178]
niSedhe'pyapUrvavidhyAdirUpeNa traividhyAzaGkA
syAdetat | na naJpadalabhyaH sarvo'pi niSedhaH parisaMkhyetyaGgIkartuM yuktam | vidhAviva niSedhe'pyapUrvavidhyAdirUpeNa traividhyasambhavAt | zakyate hi "dIkSito na juhoti" ityAdirapUrvavidhiH, "na hiMsyAt" ityAdirniyamavidhiH "nA'nuyAjAni" ityAdiH parisaMkhyeti vaktumiti cet-
tannirAsaH
maivam | parisaMkhyAzabdArthavarjanabuddhijanakasya naJo'pi parisaMkhyAtvasyA'pratyAkhyeyatayA tadyoge "na juhoti" "na hiMsyAt" ityAderapi parisaMkhyAtvasyA'nivAryatvAt | "tatra cA'nyatra ca prApte" iti lakSaNAnusAreNA'pi tasya parisaMkhyAtvaprAptiH | tadvyAvartanAya tatra vizeSaNAntaravivakSAyAzca parisaMkhyAlakSaNAkSepe dUSitatvAt | "atra hyevAvapanti" iti parisaMkhyAyAM "tatra cAnyatra ca prApte" iti yathAzrutalakSaNasyA'vyAptyabhAvAcca | tasmAdArthaH, lakSyaH, evakArapratipAdyaH, naJarthabhUtazca sarvo'pi niSedhaH parisaMkhyeti yuktam | uktaJca nyAyasudhAyAM-"parisaMkhyA varjanabuddhiH, tadartho naJAdiH parisaMkhyAzabdaH, tadyogAd brAhmaNo na hantavyaH, nekSetodyantamAdityam ityAdInAmeva zrutyA parisaMkhyAtvaM; niSedhaparyudAsavizeSavyapadezAttu vyavahArasiddherna parisaMkhyAvyapadezaH | satyameva vaditavyam ityAdInAmeva zrutyA niyamatvam | anyatra tu phalenaiva niyamaparisaMkhyAvyapadezaH" iti | evaM dRSTArthaH sarvo'pi vidhiniyamavidhiriti, sarvo'pi niSedhaH parisaMkhyeti cAGgIkRtyApUrvavidhyAdInAM lakSaNAni vibhajya darzayiSyante ityarthaH ||43||

nityaudAsyAnyakiJcidvigalitaniyataM yasya tatprAptimAtraM tatkAlaM yasya bodhaH samudayati nivRttyAtmanA'vazyameva | prAptiM bhinnasya tAbhyAM kathamapi vidadhatsyAdapUrvo vidhirnaH kurvanprAptiM tayoH syAtsa niyamaparisaMkhyAbhidhAnaH krameNa ||44 || [p.179]
niyamaparisaMkhyAlakSaNanirdezamukhena tadbhinnatvenA'pUrvavidhilakSaNanirdezaH
nityaM yadaudAsInyaM vipakSaprAptisAdhyarahitatayA nityaprAptaM yadaudAsInyam, tadanyasya kasya cidvigalitena nivRttyA niyataM yatprAptitvAvacchinnaM tanniyamavidhiSu niyamyam | kAmaM tadekamapUrvavidhilakSaNe
vyAvartyam, tathA yasya tadvidhikAle nivRttyAtmatayA dhIrniyatA, sA tasmin vidhau parisaMkhyA nAma, tadaparaM tatra vyAvartyam, tAbhyAmubhAbhyAM bhinnasya kathamapi prAptiM vidadhadvidhiH, naH asmAkaM apUrvo vidhissyAt | tayorubhayorniyamyanivRttyoH prAptiM kurvan vidhiH krameNa niyamaparisaMkhyAvidhiH syAt | niyamyaprAptiM kurvanniyamavidhiH nivRttiprAptiM kurvan parisaMkhyAvidhizca syAdityarthaH | tadetallakSaNatrayaM vivicya darzayAmaH-niyamyaparisaMkhyAnyaprAptikArI vidhirapUrvavidhiH | tatprAptikAritvaJca yathAkathaJcidvivakSitam, na tu kazcittatra vizeSo vivakSitaH | tena vidhiviSayasyA'nyasya vA prAptApoditasya,
atyantAprAptasya vA yasya kasya cit svarUpeNa kiJcidvizeSaNAntarAvacchinnatvena yena kena cidrUpeNa prakRtavidhyuddezye anyatra vA yatra kutra cit prakRtavidhyuddezye'pi sarvatra kva cidapi vA kiyatyAM prAptau satyAmasatyAM vA yathAkathaMcitprAptiM kurvannityayamartho labhyate | tena pUrvodbhAvitasarvAvyAptinirAsaH | tathA hi-agnyatigrAhyavidhiSu tAvannAvyAptiH | teSAM vidhiviSayaprAptikAritvAbhAve'pi tadAzritaguNakAmaprAptikAritvAt | agratAvidhayastu pUtIkAdividhivanniyamavidhayo vaktavyA iti, na teSvavyAptizaGkA bhavati | kintUddezyaprAptikAriNi [p.180] "tAvabrUtAmagnISomAvAjyasyaiva nAvupAMzu paurNamAsyAM yajan" iti vidhyantara iva iyamapi zaGkA labhate, uddezye'nyatra veti vizeSAnupAdAnAt | svargAyA'gnihotravidhiSu nAnAzAkhAmnAtavidhiSu ca nA'vyAptiH, teSAM svarUpeNa prAptikAritvAbhAve'pyabhyudayaziraskatvena prAptikAritvAt | nanu svarUpeNa yatprApaNIyam, tadeva prApaNIyaM bhavati na tu prAptamaprAptavizeSaNaviziSTatAmAtreNa svayaM prApaNIyatAM labhate, vivakSitavivekena vizeSaNamAtre prApaNIyatvAparyavasAnAditi cet, na, "pRSThairupatiSThate" ityatropatiSThatidhAtuvAcye samIpAvasthAne mantrakaraNakatvavAcina "upAnmantrakaraNe" (1|3|25) iti sUtravihitasyA''tmanepadasya nirvAhArthaM svarUpeNa
mantrakAryasyA'pyagnisamIpAvasthAnasya agnyabhidhAnaziraskatvaviziSTatvena viziSTarUpeNa mantrakAryatvamupapadyata iti (9|2|8) navame samarthitatvena tadvat phalabhAvanAyAH yAgakAryatvavacca svarUpeNA'prApaNIyasyA'pyaprAptavizeSaNaviziSTatayA prApaNIyatvopapatteH | tathA'pi svarUpeNA'prAptaprApaNa eva vidhivyApAra iti cet, na, tathA niyamAbhAvAt | AghArAgnihotrAdhikaraNavArtike "dadhnA juhoti" ityAdInAM prAptahomaviSayatve'pi dadhyAdiviziSTasvarUpeNa homaprAptikAritayA vidhitvasya svIkRtatvAt | anupAdeyaphalakAlAdividhInAM prAptakarmAviSayatve'pi phalakAlAdikasaMbandhitvarUpeNa tatprAptikAritayA vidhitvasya svIkRtatvAcca |
niSedharUpApUrvavidhiSvavyAptiriti tu zaGkA'navakAzaiva, niSedhAnAM parisaMkhyArUpatvasya samarthitatvAt | pratiprasavavidhiSu, sArvakAmyAdividhiSu, brahmasaMbandhavidhiSu ca nA'vyAptiH | trikAlAprAptasyeti vizeSasya, uddezye sarvatreti vizeSasya kiyatyAmapi prAptAvasatyAmeveti vizeSasya cA'vivakSitatvAt | evaJca sarvasminniyamavidhau, parisaMkhyAvidhau cA'tivyAptirApatatItyetAvadavaziSyate | tannirAsAya niyamaparisaMkhyAnyatvaM prApaNIyavizeSaNaM kRtam |

nanUktalakSaNamanupapannam, prAkRtapRSThavidhyoravyApteH | tAbhyAM vidhibhyAM prApaNIyayorbRhadrathantarasAdhanakapRSThAkhyastotrayorvikRtivizeSeSUpahavyAdiSu vidhyantarairniyamyatvAt, na ca prakRtavidhyaniyamyatvaM vivakSitam, prakRtapRSThavidhiniyamyAprasiddhayA tadanyatvoktyasambhavena, tathApyuktAvyAptitAdavasthyAditi cenna |
kiJcidviSayakaprAptitvAvacchinnaM nityaprAptaudAsInyabhinnakiJcinnivRttiniyataM yasya bhavati, tatra tanniyamyamiti vivakSitatvAt | asti ca prakRtau jyotiSTome bRhatprAptitvAvacchinnasya, rathantaraprAptitvAvacchinnasya caudAsInyabhinnavidalananivRttyA niyatebhyo'vahananaprAptyAdibhyo'nyatvam |

bRhatsAmeti, rathantarasAmeti ca nimittatayA nirdezaH, na tu sAmavizeSaviziSTayoH kratvantarayorvidhiriti siddhAntitam | dazame "ubhayasAmni naimittikam" (10|5|16|59) ityadhikaraNe ca, yeSu saMsavAdiSu bRhadrathantarapRSThasamuccayo vihitaH teSu bRhadrathantarayoH parasparavyAvartakatvAbhAvena nimittatvAbhAvAnnaimittikamagratAdvayaM "upavatIM rathantarapRSThasya pratipadaM kuryAt, agravatIM bRhatpRSThasya" iti vihitaM naimittikaM pratipadantaraM ca na kartavyamiti siddhAntitamiti cet-
